1	// Typed client for the engine FastAPI. SAME-ORIGIN relative `/api/*` (the appliance serves
2	// UI + engine behind one origin; in dev Vite proxies `/api` → :8000). Every call carries a
3	// correlation id (header + logs) so a journey is traceable end to end.
4	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
5	import type { RallySegment, QueryResponse, CompileResponse, EngineConfig, ProcessResponse, JobStatus } from "./types";
6	
7	const BASE = "/api";
8	
9	export class ApiError extends Error {
10	  readonly cid?: string;
11	  readonly status?: number;
12	  readonly body?: string;
13	  constructor(message: string, opts: { cid?: string; status?: number; body?: string; cause?: unknown } = {}) {
14	    super(message);
15	    this.name = "ApiError";
16	    this.cid = opts.cid;
17	    this.status = opts.status;
18	    this.body = opts.body;
19	    if (opts.cause !== undefined) (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = opts.cause;
20	  }
21	}
22	
23	interface RequestOpts {
24	  method?: string;
25	  body?: string;
26	  headers?: Record<string, string>;
27	  /** Correlation id to thread through this call; auto-generated if absent. */
28	  cid?: string;
29	}
30	
31	async function request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOpts = {}): Promise<T> {
32	  const cid = opts.cid ?? newCorrelationId();
33	  const url = `${BASE}${path}`;
34	  const method = opts.method ?? "GET";
35	  log("info", "api.request", { cid, method, url });
36	
37	  let res: Response;
38	  try {
39	    res = await fetch(url, {
40	      method,
41	      body: opts.body,
42	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-correlation-id": cid, ...(opts.headers ?? {}) },
43	    });
44	  } catch (err) {
45	    // Loud failure, never silent (LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §5).
46	    log("error", "api.network_error", { cid, method, url, error: String(err) });
47	    throw new ApiError(`Network error calling ${method} ${url}`, { cid, cause: err });
48	  }
49	
50	  if (!res.ok) {
51	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
52	    log("error", "api.http_error", { cid, method, url, status: res.status, body: body.slice(0, 500) });
53	    throw new ApiError(`${method} ${url} failed: ${res.status}`, { cid, status: res.status, body });
54	  }
55	
56	  const data = (await res.json()) as T;
57	  log("info", "api.response", { cid, method, url, status: res.status });
58	  return data;
59	}
60	
61	/** Submit a video (a `gs://`/`gcs://`/`s3://` bucket URI, or a path on the engine host) for
62	 *  processing. POST /api/process {video_path} → 202 {job_id, ...} [main.py:260]. Fast-fails 400
63	 *  (unsupported scheme / out-of-allowed-root) or 404 (missing LOCAL file) — surfaced as ApiError. */
64	export async function processVideo(videoPath: string, cid?: string): Promise<ProcessResponse> {
65	  return request<ProcessResponse>("/process", {
66	    method: "POST",
67	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_path: videoPath }),
68	    cid,
69	  });
70	}
71	
72	/** Poll a submitted job. GET /api/jobs/{job_id} [main.py:303]. 404 ⇒ unknown job_id. */
73	export async function getJob(jobId: string, cid?: string): Promise<JobStatus> {
74	  return request<JobStatus>(`/jobs/${encodeURIComponent(jobId)}`, { method: "GET", cid });
75	}
76	
77	/** List a video's detected rallies. POST /api/query {video_id} → {play_segments} [main.py:331]. */
78	export async function listRallies(videoId: string, cid?: string): Promise<RallySegment[]> {
79	  const data = await request<QueryResponse>("/query", {
80	    method: "POST",
81	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_id: videoId }),
82	    cid,
83	  });
84	  return data.play_segments ?? [];
85	}
86	
87	/** Stitch selected rallies into one reel. POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids, output_filename} [main.py:342]. */
88	export async function compileReel(
89	  videoId: string,
90	  segmentIds: number[],
91	  outputFilename: string,
92	  cid?: string,
93	): Promise<CompileResponse> {
94	  return request<CompileResponse>("/compile", {
95	    method: "POST",
96	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_id: videoId, segment_ids: segmentIds, output_filename: outputFilename }),
97	    cid,
98	  });
99	}
100	
101	/** The stream/download URL for a compiled reel. GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename} [main.py:307]. */
102	export function highlightUrl(videoId: string, filename: string): string {
103	  return `${BASE}/highlights/${encodeURIComponent(videoId)}/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`;
104	}
105	
106	/** Read engine config (for the stitching floor). GET /api/config [main.py:121]. */
107	export async function getConfig(cid?: string): Promise<EngineConfig> {
108	  return request<EngineConfig>("/config", { method: "GET", cid });
109	}
110	
111	/** The shot-count floor /api/compile silently enforces — the UI must warn, not hide [main.py:362]. */
112	export function minShotCount(cfg: EngineConfig): number | undefined {
113	  return cfg.stitching?.min_shot_count;
114	}

### 92. user (2026-06-21T14:58:37.858Z)

1	// Drives the front-half ingest: submit a video to POST /api/process, then poll GET /api/jobs/{id}
2	// until it finishes, and hand the resulting video_id to the existing rally-loading back-half.
3	//
4	// Kept i18n-FREE (returns stable IngestErrorCodes, not prose) so it unit-tests without a
5	// translation context — IngestPanel maps codes to localized strings. The poll loop is a RECURSIVE
6	// setTimeout (never setInterval): each tick waits for the prior getJob to resolve, so a slow engine
7	// can't pile up overlapping requests. It backs off, gives up after a ceiling, and is fully
8	// cancellable on unmount / user-cancel / a superseding submit.
9	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
10	import { getJob, processVideo } from "../api/client";
11	import { classifyError, type IngestError } from "../lib/errors";
12	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
13	import type { JobStatus } from "../api/types";
14	
15	const POLL_MIN_MS = 1000;
16	const POLL_MAX_MS = 5000;
17	const POLL_BACKOFF = 1.5;
18	const POLL_CEILING_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000; // a wedged 'running' job won't poll forever
19	
20	export type IngestPhase =
21	  | { status: "idle" }
22	  | { status: "submitting" }
23	  | { status: "polling"; progress: string | null }
24	  | { status: "done"; videoId: string }
25	  | { status: "error"; error: IngestError };
26	
27	interface Run {
28	  cancelled: boolean;
29	  timer?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
30	}
31	
32	export function useIngestJob(onLoaded?: (videoId: string) => void) {
33	  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<IngestPhase>({ status: "idle" });
34	  const runRef = useRef<Run | null>(null);
35	  // Keep the latest onLoaded without making submit depend on it (a parent re-render won't restart polls).
36	  const onLoadedRef = useRef(onLoaded);
37	  onLoadedRef.current = onLoaded;
38	
39	  const stop = useCallback(() => {
40	    const run = runRef.current;
41	    if (run) {
42	      run.cancelled = true;
43	      if (run.timer) clearTimeout(run.timer);
44	    }
45	    runRef.current = null;
46	  }, []);
47	
48	  // Cancel any in-flight poll loop when the component unmounts (App.tsx:80-82 alive-flag idiom).
49	  useEffect(() => stop, [stop]);
50	
51	  const submit = useCallback(
52	    (rawUri: string) => {
53	      const uri = rawUri.trim();
54	      if (!uri) {
55	        setPhase({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalidUri" } });
56	        return;
57	      }
58	
59	      stop(); // supersede any prior run
60	      const run: Run = { cancelled: false };
61	      runRef.current = run;
62	
63	      const cid = newCorrelationId();
64	      const startedAt = Date.now();
65	      setPhase({ status: "submitting" });
66	      log("info", "ingest.submit_start", { cid, uri });
67	
68	      const fail = (error: IngestError, event: string) => {
69	        if (run.cancelled) return;
70	        setPhase({ status: "error", error });
71	        const level = error.code === "timeout" || error.code === "emptyResult" ? "warn" : "error";
72	        log(level, event, { cid, uri, code: error.code, detail: error.detail });
73	      };
74	
75	      const poll = (jobId: string, delay: number) => {
76	        run.timer = setTimeout(() => {
77	          void (async () => {
78	            if (run.cancelled) return;
79	            let job: JobStatus;
80	            try {
81	              job = await getJob(jobId, cid);
82	            } catch (err) {
83	              fail(classifyError(err), "ingest.poll_error");
84	              return;
85	            }
86	            if (run.cancelled) return;
87	
88	            if (job.status === "done") {
89	              const videoId = job.video_id ?? job.result?.video_id ?? null;
90	              // Two status layers: a `done` job with a `failed` pipeline verdict (or no video_id) is
91	              // a success-shaped FAILURE — never load it, never hang.
92	              if (job.result?.status === "failed" || !videoId) {
93	                fail({ code: "emptyResult" }, "ingest.empty_result");
94	                return;
95	              }
96	              setPhase({ status: "done", videoId });
97	              log("info", "ingest.done", { cid, uri, video_id: videoId });
98	              onLoadedRef.current?.(videoId);
99	              return;
100	            }
101	            if (job.status === "failed") {
102	              fail({ code: "engine", detail: job.error ?? "job failed" }, "ingest.job_failed");
103	              return;
104	            }
105	            // queued | running → reflect coarse progress (free-text phase) and keep polling.
106	            setPhase({ status: "polling", progress: job.progress ?? null });
107	            if (Date.now() - startedAt > POLL_CEILING_MS) {
108	              fail({ code: "timeout" }, "ingest.poll_timeout");
109	              return;
110	            }
111	            poll(jobId, Math.min(delay * POLL_BACKOFF, POLL_MAX_MS));
112	          })();
113	        }, delay);
114	      };
115	
116	      void (async () => {
117	        let jobId: string;
118	        try {
119	          const resp = await processVideo(uri, cid);
120	          jobId = resp.job_id;
121	        } catch (err) {
122	          fail(classifyError(err), "ingest.submit_error");
123	          return;
124	        }
125	        if (run.cancelled) return;
126	        log("info", "ingest.queued", { cid, uri, job_id: jobId });
127	        setPhase({ status: "polling", progress: null });
128	        poll(jobId, POLL_MIN_MS);
129	      })();
130	    },
131	    [stop],
132	  );
133	
134	  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
135	    stop();
136	    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
137	    log("info", "ingest.cancel", {});
138	  }, [stop]);
139	
140	  const reset = useCallback(() => {
141	    stop();
142	    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
143	  }, [stop]);
144	
145	  const busy = phase.status === "submitting" || phase.status === "polling";
146	  return { phase, submit, cancel, reset, busy };
147	}
148

25	  });

28	    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("x", { status: 502 }))).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
29	  });
30	  it("a 5xx WITH a detail still surfaces the engine's message", () => {
31	    const err = new ApiError("x", { status: 500, body: JSON.stringify({ detail: "failed to persist job record" }) });
32	    expect(classifyError(err)).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "failed to persist job record" });
33	  });
34	  it("a 4xx with no body keeps the status (a client-side mistake, not unreachable)", () => {
35	    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("x", { status: 404 }))).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "HTTP 404" });
36	  });
37	  it("a non-ApiError → engine + the stringified error", () => {
38	    expect(classifyError(new Error("boom"))).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "Error: boom" });
39	  });
40	});

1	"""Chunked upload (B2, PR-2) — extracted from backend.main into its own router.
2	
3	Browser uploads arrive in sequential parts ≤ security.max_part_mb (free-tier edge
4	proxies cap request bodies at ~100 MB — HOSTING_PLAN §2), are assembled server-side
5	under security.upload_dir, then submitted to /api/process by the returned path.
6	Upload state is in-process by design (this is the single-box alpha): a server restart
7	aborts partial uploads and the client re-inits. Per-user partitioning of upload_dir
8	arrives with PR-3 identity scoping.
9	
10	The startup config lives on ``backend.main.global_config`` (set ONLY in ``main()``).
11	``_upload_cfg`` resolves it through the ``backend.main`` module object so it always
12	reads the live value the server set at startup (and so tests that monkeypatch
13	``backend.main.global_config`` flow through unchanged).
14	"""
15	import os
16	import threading
17	import uuid
18	from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
19	
20	from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
21	from pydantic import BaseModel
22	
23	from backend.utils.hashing import compute_video_id  # canonical file-identity hashing
24	from backend.utils.paths import safe_output_filename
25	
26	router = APIRouter()
27	
28	_uploads: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
29	_uploads_lock = threading.Lock()
30	
31	
32	class UploadInitRequest(BaseModel):
33	    filename: str
34	    total_size_bytes: Optional[int] = None
35	
36	
37	class UploadCompleteRequest(BaseModel):
38	    total_parts: int
39	
40	
41	def _upload_cfg():
42	    # Resolved lazily (import inside the function) to read the LIVE startup config that
43	    # main() sets on the module global, and to avoid a circular import at module load
44	    # (main.py imports this router; this module must not import main at import time).
45	    import backend.main as _main
46	    sec = getattr(_main.global_config, "security", None)
47	    upload_dir = getattr(sec, "upload_dir", None) or "output/uploads"
48	    max_bytes = int(getattr(sec, "max_upload_mb", 4096) or 4096) * 1024 * 1024
49	    part_bytes = int(getattr(sec, "max_part_mb", 95) or 95) * 1024 * 1024
50	    exts = [e.lower().lstrip(".") for e in (getattr(sec, "allowed_upload_extensions", None) or ["mp4", "mov"])]
51	    return upload_dir, max_bytes, part_bytes, exts
52	
53	
54	def _abort_upload(upload_id: str) -> None:
55	    with _uploads_lock:
56	        st = _uploads.pop(upload_id, None)
57	    if st:
58	        try:
59	            os.remove(st["tmp_path"])
60	        except OSError:
61	            pass
62	
63	
64	@router.post("/api/upload/init")
65	def upload_init(req: UploadInitRequest):
66	    """Start a chunked upload. Returns upload_id; PUT sequential parts next."""
67	    upload_dir, max_bytes, part_bytes, exts = _upload_cfg()
68	    try:
69	        name = safe_output_filename(req.filename)
70	    except ValueError as e:
71	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e)) from e
72	    ext = os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower().lstrip(".")
73	    if ext not in exts:
74	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400,
75	                            detail=f"Extension '.{ext}' not allowed. Allowed: {sorted(exts)}")
76	    if req.total_size_bytes is not None and req.total_size_bytes > max_bytes:
77	        raise HTTPException(status_code=413,
78	                            detail=f"File exceeds the {max_bytes // (1024 * 1024)} MB upload limit.")
79	    os.makedirs(upload_dir, exist_ok=True)
80	    upload_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
81	    tmp_path = os.path.join(upload_dir, f".partial-{upload_id}")
82	    open(tmp_path, "wb").close()
83	    with _uploads_lock:
84	        _uploads[upload_id] = {"filename": name, "tmp_path": tmp_path, "parts": 0, "bytes": 0}
85	    return {"upload_id": upload_id, "max_part_mb": part_bytes // (1024 * 1024),
86	            "next_part": f"/api/upload/{upload_id}/part/0"}
87	
88	
89	@router.put("/api/upload/{upload_id}/part/{index}")
90	async def upload_part(upload_id: str, index: int, request: Request):
91	    """Append one part (raw bytes body). Parts are strictly sequential (0,1,2,...)."""
92	    _, max_bytes, part_bytes, _ = _upload_cfg()
93	    with _uploads_lock:
94	        st = _uploads.get(upload_id)
95	    if st is None:
96	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Unknown upload_id '{upload_id}'")
97	    if index != st["parts"]:
98	        raise HTTPException(status_code=409,
99	                            detail=f"Out-of-order part: expected {st['parts']}, got {index} (parts are sequential)")
100	
101	    received = 0
102	    overflow = None
103	    with open(st["tmp_path"], "ab") as fh:
104	        async for chunk in request.stream():
105	            received += len(chunk)
106	            if received > part_bytes:
107	                overflow = f"Part exceeds the {part_bytes // (1024 * 1024)} MB per-part limit."
108	                break
109	            if st["bytes"] + received > max_bytes:
110	                overflow = f"Upload exceeds the {max_bytes // (1024 * 1024)} MB total limit."
111	                break
112	            fh.write(chunk)
113	    if overflow:
114	        _abort_upload(upload_id)
115	        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail=overflow + " Upload aborted — re-init to retry.")
116	
117	    with _uploads_lock:
118	        st["parts"] += 1
119	        st["bytes"] += received
120	    return {"upload_id": upload_id, "received_parts": st["parts"], "received_bytes": st["bytes"]}
121	
122	
123	@router.post("/api/upload/{upload_id}/complete")
124	def upload_complete(upload_id: str, req: UploadCompleteRequest):
125	    """Finalize: verify part count, move into place, return {path, video_id} for /api/process."""
126	    upload_dir, _, _, _ = _upload_cfg()
127	    with _uploads_lock:
128	        st = _uploads.get(upload_id)
129	    if st is None:
130	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Unknown upload_id '{upload_id}'")
131	    if st["parts"] == 0 or st["parts"] != req.total_parts:
132	        raise HTTPException(status_code=409,
133	                            detail=f"Expected {req.total_parts} part(s), received {st['parts']}.")
134	    final_path = os.path.join(upload_dir, st["filename"])
135	    if os.path.exists(final_path):  # never overwrite an earlier upload with the same name
136	        stem, ext = os.path.splitext(st["filename"])
137	        final_path = os.path.join(upload_dir, f"{stem}-{upload_id[:8]}{ext}")
138	    os.replace(st["tmp_path"], final_path)
139	    with _uploads_lock:
140	        _uploads.pop(upload_id, None)
141	    video_id = compute_video_id(final_path)
142	    return {"path": os.path.abspath(final_path), "video_id": video_id,
143	            "size_bytes": os.path.getsize(final_path),
144	            "next": "POST /api/process with this path"}
145	
146	
147	@router.delete("/api/upload/{upload_id}")
148	def upload_abort(upload_id: str):
149	    """Abort an in-flight upload and delete its partial file."""
150	    with _uploads_lock:
151	        known = upload_id in _uploads
152	    if not known:
153	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Unknown upload_id '{upload_id}'")
154	    _abort_upload(upload_id)
155	    return {"status": "aborted", "upload_id": upload_id}

1	// Always-on VM host for the KhelSutra site — dependency-free (Node 22+ only).
2	// Serves public/ and POST /api/request-access into SQLite (node:sqlite), using the
3	// SAME handler logic as the Cloudflare Worker (src/index.js). This is the portability
4	// fallback "if Cloudflare acts out" — see docs/PORTABILITY.md.
5	//
6	//   PORT=8080 DB_PATH=/var/lib/khelsutra/access.db node server.js
7	//
8	import { createServer } from "node:http";
9	import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
10	import { join, extname, normalize } from "node:path";
11	import { DatabaseSync } from "node:sqlite";
12	import { handleRequest } from "./src/index.js";
13	import { sqliteStore } from "./src/store.js";
14	import { noopNotify } from "./src/notify.js";
15	
16	const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080;
17	const ROOT = import.meta.dirname;
18	const PUBLIC = join(ROOT, "public");
19	const DB_PATH = process.env.DB_PATH || join(ROOT, "access.db");
20	
21	const db = new DatabaseSync(DB_PATH);
22	db.exec(await readFile(join(ROOT, "schema.sql"), "utf8")); // CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS — idempotent
23	const store = sqliteStore(db);
24	const notify = noopNotify(); // VM host: no email yet (add SMTP later — see docs/PORTABILITY.md)
25	
26	const TYPES = {
27	  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8", ".png": "image/png", ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
28	  ".css": "text/css", ".js": "text/javascript", ".ico": "image/x-icon", ".txt": "text/plain",
29	  ".mp4": "video/mp4", ".webm": "video/webm", ".gif": "image/gif", ".jpg": "image/jpeg", ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
30	};
31	
32	async function toRequest(req) {
33	  const url = `http://${req.headers.host || "localhost"}${req.url}`;
34	  let body;
35	  if (req.method !== "GET" && req.method !== "HEAD") {
36	    const chunks = [];
37	    for await (const c of req) chunks.push(c);
38	    body = Buffer.concat(chunks);
39	  }
40	  return new Request(url, { method: req.method, headers: req.headers, body });
41	}
42	
43	async function send(res, response) {
44	  res.statusCode = response.status;
45	  for (const [k, v] of response.headers) res.setHeader(k, v);
46	  res.end(Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer()));
47	}
48	
49	async function serveStatic(pathname, res) {
50	  const rel = pathname === "/" ? "index.html"
51	    : pathname === "/capture" ? "capture.html"
52	    : pathname === "/own-model" ? "own-model.html"
53	    : pathname === "/flyer" ? "flyer.html"
54	    : pathname === "/flyer-kn" ? "flyer-kn.html"
55	    : pathname === "/flyer-hi" ? "flyer-hi.html"
56	    : pathname.replace(/^\/+/, "");
57	  const file = normalize(join(PUBLIC, rel));
58	  if (!file.startsWith(PUBLIC)) { res.statusCode = 403; return res.end("forbidden"); }
59	  try {
60	    const data = await readFile(file);
61	    res.setHeader("content-type", TYPES[extname(file)] || "application/octet-stream");
62	    res.end(data);
63	  } catch {
64	    res.statusCode = 404;
65	    res.end("not found");
66	  }
67	}
68	
69	createServer(async (req, res) => {
70	  try {
71	    const pathname = decodeURIComponent(new URL(`http://x${req.url}`).pathname);
72	    if (pathname === "/api/request-access") {
73	      if (req.method !== "POST") { res.statusCode = 405; return res.end('{"ok":false,"error":"method not allowed"}'); }
74	      return await send(res, await handleRequest(await toRequest(req), store, notify));
75	    }
76	    return await serveStatic(pathname, res);
77	  } catch (err) {
78	    res.statusCode = 500;
79	    res.end('{"ok":false,"error":"server error"}');
80	    console.error(err);
81	  }
82	}).listen(PORT, () => console.log(`khelsutra site listening on :${PORT}  (sqlite: ${DB_PATH})`));

### 139. assistant (2026-06-21T15:08:53.111Z)

// The ONE place a classified error (lib/errors.ts) becomes user-facing text. Both surfaces use it:
// the ingest flow (IngestPanel) and the build flow (App.onBuild → CompileResult), so a 5xx / engine-
// down failure reads the same friendly way everywhere instead of a raw "HTTP 500" / fetch message.
//
// `engine` carries the engine's OWN message (the most specific) → shown verbatim, never translated.
// Every other code maps to a friendly localized line in the shared `errors.*` namespace. Kept out of
// errors.ts so that module stays i18n-free and unit-testable without a translation context.
import type { TFunction } from "i18next";
import type { IngestError } from "./errors";

export function errorText(t: TFunction, e: IngestError): string {
  if (e.code === "engine") return e.detail ?? t("errors.engineGeneric");
  return t(`errors.${e.code}`);
}

### 148. assistant (2026-06-21T15:09:50.736Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// errorText is the single mapping from a classified error → user-facing text, shared by the ingest
// and build flows (A5). These pin: the engine message passes through verbatim, a detail-less engine
// error degrades to a friendly localized line (never a raw key), and every non-engine code resolves
// to a real catalog string in the shared `errors.*` namespace.
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll } from "vitest";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import { errorText } from "./errorText";
import type { IngestErrorCode } from "./errors";

beforeAll(async () => {
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
});

describe("errorText", () => {
  it("an engine error shows the engine's own detail verbatim (most specific message)", () => {
    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "engine", detail: "min_shot_count too low" })).toBe("min_shot_count too low");
  });

  it("an engine error with no detail degrades to a friendly localized line (not a raw key)", () => {
    const s = errorText(i18n.t, { code: "engine" });
    expect(s).toBe(i18n.t("errors.engineGeneric"));
    expect(s).not.toMatch(/^errors\./);
    expect(s.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("networkError maps to the friendly 'is it running?' line", () => {
    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "networkError" })).toMatch(/is it running/i);
  });

  it("every non-engine code resolves to a real catalog string (never a raw key)", () => {
    for (const code of ["invalidUri", "networkError", "emptyResult", "timeout"] as IngestErrorCode[]) {
      const s = errorText(i18n.t, { code });
      expect(s, code).not.toMatch(/^errors\./);
      expect(s.length, code).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
  });

  it("localizes per the active locale (Kannada network message)", async () => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage("kn");
    expect(errorText(i18n.t, { code: "networkError" })).toBe("ಎಂಜಿನ್ ತಲುಪಲಾಗುತ್ತಿಲ್ಲ. ಅದು ಚಾಲನೆಯಲ್ಲಿದೆಯೇ?");
    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
  });
});

### 151. assistant (2026-06-21T15:10:46.464Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// A5 — the BUILD path (App.onBuild → CompileResult) now uses the SAME friendly mapping as ingest
// (classifyError → errorText), so a non-technical user never sees a raw "Network error calling POST
// /api/compile" / "HTTP 500". A real engine 4xx still surfaces the engine's own detail (the most
// specific, actionable message). Drives the real <App /> with a loaded video + a stubbed engine.
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import App from "../App";
import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";

const RALLIES: RallySegment[] = [
  { id: 1, start_time: 10, end_time: 20, duration: 10, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 12 } },
  { id: 2, start_time: 30, end_time: 45, duration: 15, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 14 } },
];

const httpOk = (status: number, json: unknown) => ({
  ok: status >= 200 && status < 300, status, json: async () => json, text: async () => JSON.stringify(json),
});
const httpErr = (status: number, body: string) => ({
  ok: false, status, json: async () => JSON.parse(body), text: async () => body,
});

// /api/query → rallies; /api/config → 500 (cfg unavailable → no floor warning); /api/compile → caller-supplied.
function mockFetch(compile: () => unknown) {
  return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const u = String(url);
    const method = init?.method ?? "GET";
    if (u === "/api/query" && method === "POST") return httpOk(200, { play_segments: RALLIES });
    if (u === "/api/config") return httpErr(500, "");
    if (u === "/api/compile" && method === "POST") return compile();
    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${method} ${u}`);
  }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  window.history.pushState({}, "", "?video=vidX"); // job-tethered: load a real video's rallies
  window.localStorage.clear();
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
});

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  window.history.pushState({}, "", "/");
});

// Rallies load all-selected (count 2) → the Build button label proves the load completed.
const loadAndBuild = async () => {
  render(<App />);
  fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: /build reel \(2\)/i }));
};

describe("A5: friendly build-error mapping", () => {
  it("a network / engine-down compile failure shows the friendly 'is it running?' line", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", mockFetch(() => {
      throw new Error("offline");
    }));
    await loadAndBuild();
    expect(await screen.findByText(/can't reach khelsutra/i)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/network error calling/i)).toBeNull(); // the raw client message is gone
  });

  it("a real engine 4xx surfaces the engine's own detail verbatim", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", mockFetch(async () => httpErr(400, JSON.stringify({ detail: "rally below min_shot_count" }))));
    await loadAndBuild();
    expect(await screen.findByText(/rally below min_shot_count/i)).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

### 196. assistant (2026-06-21T15:23:24.479Z)

// A status indicator that is ALWAYS icon + text — never icon-only (HCI4D guidance for non-tech-savvy
// users, FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL §4: pair every icon with a word, don't rely on color alone). Used
// for the ingest "working / couldn't process" states and the build "stitching / ready / failed" states.
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

type Tone = "info" | "success" | "error";

const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
  info: "bg-ink/5 text-ink/70",
  success: "bg-green/15 text-green",
  error: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
};

interface Props {
  /** A glyph or the <Spinner/> — decorative; the label carries the meaning for screen readers. */
  icon: ReactNode;
  label: string;
  tone?: Tone;
}

export function StatusChip({ icon, label, tone = "info" }: Props) {
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium ${TONE[tone]}`}>
      <span aria-hidden="true" className="inline-flex">
        {icon}
      </span>
      <span>{label}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

/** Indeterminate spinner — the engine reports coarse phase WORDS, not a percentage, so progress is a
 *  spinner + the phase label rather than a misleading progress bar. */
export function Spinner() {
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-current/30 border-t-current"
      aria-hidden="true"
    />
  );
}

### 213. assistant (2026-06-21T15:27:04.002Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// A8 — honest expectations for non-tech users on slow uplinks: background-processing reassurance and
// status shown as icon+TEXT chips (never icon-only), on BOTH the ingest progress and the build state.
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent, act } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import { CompileResult } from "./CompileResult";
import { IngestPanel } from "./IngestPanel";
import type { JobStatus } from "../api/types";

beforeEach(async () => {
  window.localStorage.clear();
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
});

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("A8: build state shows status + reassurance (CompileResult)", () => {
  it("building → a 'stitching…' chip + a reassurance line (and nothing to dismiss yet)", () => {
    render(<CompileResult build={{ status: "building" }} onDismiss={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("Stitching your reel…")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/usually takes under a minute/i)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: "Dismiss" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("done → a ✓ icon paired with the 'ready' text (icon+text, not icon-only)", () => {
    render(<CompileResult build={{ status: "done", url: "/x", filename: "r.mp4" }} onDismiss={() => {}} />);
    const h = screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 2 });
    expect(h.textContent).toContain("✓");
    expect(h.textContent).toContain("Your reel is ready");
  });

  it("error → a ⚠ icon paired with 'failed' + the message", () => {
    render(<CompileResult build={{ status: "error", error: "boom" }} onDismiss={() => {}} />);
    const h = screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 2 });
    expect(h.textContent).toContain("⚠");
    expect(screen.getByText("boom")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("A8: ingest progress reassurance + status chips (IngestPanel)", () => {
  const httpOk = (s: number, j: unknown) => ({ ok: s >= 200 && s < 300, status: s, json: async () => j, text: async () => JSON.stringify(j) });
  function fetchSeq(process: unknown, jobs: JobStatus[]) {
    let i = 0;
    return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
      const u = String(url);
      if (u === "/api/process" && (init?.method ?? "GET") === "POST") return process;
      if (u.startsWith("/api/jobs/")) {
        const b = jobs[Math.min(i, jobs.length - 1)];
        i += 1;
        return httpOk(200, b);
      }
      throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${u}`);
    }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
  }

  it("while processing → the working chip + 'safe to wait' reassurance", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchSeq(httpOk(202, { job_id: "j", status: "queued" }), [
      { job_id: "j", status: "running", progress: "segmenting" },
    ]));
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/video location/i), { target: { value: "gs://b/x.mp4" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /process video/i }));
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // submit → polling
    });
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000); // running tick → progress word
    });
    expect(screen.getByText(/processing your video/i)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText(/safe to wait/i)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("on failure → a ⚠ 'Couldn't process' status chip alongside the friendly message", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => {
      throw new Error("offline");
    }) as unknown as typeof fetch);
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/video location/i), { target: { value: "gs://b/x.mp4" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /process video/i }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("Couldn't process")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(await screen.findByText(/is it running/i)).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

+++ b/web/src/components/StatusChip.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+// A status indicator that is ALWAYS icon + text — never icon-only (HCI4D guidance for non-tech-savvy
+// users, FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL §4: pair every icon with a word, don't rely on color alone). Used
+// for the ingest "working / couldn't process" states and the build "stitching / ready / failed" states.
+import type { ReactNode } from "react";
+
+type Tone = "info" | "success" | "error";
+
+const TONE: Record<Tone, string> = {
+  info: "bg-ink/5 text-ink/70",
+  success: "bg-green/15 text-green",
+  error: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
+};
+
+interface Props {
+  /** A glyph or the <Spinner/> — decorative; the label carries the meaning for screen readers. */
+  icon: ReactNode;
+  label: string;
+  tone?: Tone;
+}
+
+export function StatusChip({ icon, label, tone = "info" }: Props) {
+  return (
+    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium ${TONE[tone]}`}>
+      <span aria-hidden="true" className="inline-flex">
+        {icon}
+      </span>
+      <span>{label}</span>
+    </span>
+  );
+}
+
+/** Indeterminate spinner — the engine reports coarse phase WORDS, not a percentage, so progress is a
+ *  spinner + the phase label rather than a misleading progress bar. */
+export function Spinner() {
+  return (
+    <span
+      className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-ink/20 border-t-ink/70"
+      aria-hidden="true"
+    />
+  );
+}

### 267. assistant (2026-06-21T16:22:57.913Z)

// Drives the in-UI file UPLOAD half of ingest (tracker T4): chunk a local file up to the engine's
// /api/upload/* endpoints, report byte progress, then hand the returned server PATH to the existing
// processing back-half (useIngestJob.submit). Alpha users have a FILE, not a gs:// URI — this is the
// keystone that lets them point the tool at it from the browser.
//
// Kept i18n-FREE (returns stable IngestErrorCodes via classifyError, not prose) so it unit-tests
// without a translation context — IngestPanel maps codes to localized strings via errorText. Abort
// is an AbortController: cancel/unmount/a superseding pick stops the in-flight upload and the
// orchestrator best-effort DELETEs the server-side partial.
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { uploadFile } from "../api/client";
import { classifyError, type IngestError } from "../lib/errors";
import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";

export type UploadPhase =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "uploading"; pct: number; filename: string }
  | { status: "error"; error: IngestError };

export function useUpload(onComplete: (path: string) => void) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<UploadPhase>({ status: "idle" });
  const ctrlRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);
  // Keep the latest onComplete without making start depend on it (a parent re-render won't restart).
  const onCompleteRef = useRef(onComplete);
  onCompleteRef.current = onComplete;

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    ctrlRef.current?.abort();
    ctrlRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  // Abort any in-flight upload on unmount.
  useEffect(() => stop, [stop]);

  const start = useCallback(
    (file: File) => {
      // A 0-byte "video" is never valid — reject before touching the network (reuse invalidUri copy).
      if (!file || file.size === 0) {
        setPhase({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalidUri" } });
        return;
      }

      stop(); // supersede any prior upload
      const ctrl = new AbortController();
      ctrlRef.current = ctrl;
      const cid = newCorrelationId();
      setPhase({ status: "uploading", pct: 0, filename: file.name });
      log("info", "upload.submit_start", { cid, filename: file.name, size: file.size });

      void (async () => {
        try {
          const res = await uploadFile(file, {
            cid,
            signal: ctrl.signal,
            onProgress: ({ sentBytes, totalBytes }) => {
              if (ctrl.signal.aborted) return;
              const pct = totalBytes > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((sentBytes / totalBytes) * 100)) : 0;
              setPhase({ status: "uploading", pct, filename: file.name });
            },
          });
          if (ctrl.signal.aborted) return;
          setPhase({ status: "idle" }); // hand off — the engine processing phase takes over
          onCompleteRef.current(res.path);
        } catch (err) {
          // User-cancelled (abort) is silent, not an error.
          if (ctrl.signal.aborted || (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "AbortError")) return;
          setPhase({ status: "error", error: classifyError(err) });
          log("error", "upload.failed", { cid, filename: file.name, error: String(err) });
        }
      })();
    },
    [stop],
  );

  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
    stop();
    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
    log("info", "upload.cancel", {});
  }, [stop]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    stop();
    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
  }, [stop]);

  const busy = phase.status === "uploading";
  return { phase, start, cancel, reset, busy };
}

1	// The front-half ingest screen (appliance S1/S2, tracker B-P2). Point the tool at a video — a
2	// `gs://` bucket URI or a path on the engine host — and it submits to POST /api/process, shows
3	// live progress while polling GET /api/jobs/{id}, and hands the resulting video_id to the existing
4	// rally-selection back-half (App lifts ?video=<id>). Honest by construction: the engine reports
5	// coarse phase WORDS (not a %), and a success-shaped failure (done but no rallies) is shown, never
6	// silently treated as a load.
7	import { useState } from "react";
8	import type { FormEvent } from "react";
9	import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
10	import { useIngestJob } from "../hooks/useIngestJob";
11	import { errorText } from "../lib/errorText";
12	import { StatusChip, Spinner } from "./StatusChip";
13	
14	interface Props {
15	  /** Called with the engine's video_id once a submitted job finishes successfully. */
16	  onLoaded: (videoId: string) => void;
17	}
18	
19	export function IngestPanel({ onLoaded }: Props) {
20	  const { t } = useTranslation();
21	  const [uri, setUri] = useState("");

25	    e.preventDefault();

27	  };

29	  // Shared mapping with the build flow (lib/errorText): `engine` failures carry the engine's own
30	  // message (a 400 scheme/path detail, a worker error); every other code maps to a friendly
31	  // localized line — the network case especially, since this is the first screen a fresh user hits
32	  // and "is the engine running?" must read plainly.
33	  const errorMsg = phase.status === "error" ? errorText(t, phase.error) : null;
34	
35	  return (
36	    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-black/10 bg-white p-5" data-testid="ingest-panel">
37	      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{t("ingest.title")}</h2>
38	      <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink/60">{t("ingest.intro")}</p>
39	
40	      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
41	        <input
42	          type="text"
43	          value={uri}
44	          onChange={(e) => setUri(e.target.value)}
45	          placeholder={t("ingest.uriPlaceholder")}
46	          aria-label={t("ingest.uriAriaLabel")}
47	          disabled={busy}
48	          className="min-h-11 min-w-[18rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green disabled:opacity-50"
49	        />
50	        <button
51	          type="submit"
52	          disabled={busy}
53	          className="min-h-11 rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
54	        >
55	          {phase.status === "submitting" ? t("ingest.submitting") : t("ingest.submit")}
56	        </button>
57	        {busy && (
58	          <button
59	            type="button"
60	            onClick={cancel}
61	            className="min-h-11 rounded-xl border border-black/15 px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:border-green"
62	          >
63	            {t("ingest.cancel")}
64	          </button>
65	        )}
66	      </form>
67	
68	      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">{t("ingest.hint")}</p>
69

71	          (A8). Indeterminate: the engine reports phase words ("hashing", "segmenting"), not a %. */}

82	                  : "")

86	            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("ingest.reassure")}</p>
87	          </div>
88	        )}
89	        {phase.status === "error" && (

91	            <StatusChip tone="error" icon="⚠" label={t("ingest.statusFailed")} />
92	            <p className="mt-2">{errorMsg}</p>

### 272. assistant (2026-06-21T16:25:00.277Z)

// The front-half ingest screen (appliance S1/S2; tracker B-P2 + T4). Two ways to point the tool at a
// video: UPLOAD a local file (primary — alpha users have a file, not a URI; chunked to the engine's
// /api/upload/* endpoints) or paste a `gs://` bucket URI / host path (secondary). Both converge on
// the SAME back-half: POST /api/process → poll GET /api/jobs/{id} → hand the video_id to App. Honest
// by construction: the engine reports coarse phase WORDS (not a %), and a success-shaped failure
// (done but no rallies) is shown, never silently treated as a load.
import { useState } from "react";
import type { ChangeEvent, FormEvent } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useIngestJob } from "../hooks/useIngestJob";
import { useUpload } from "../hooks/useUpload";
import { errorText } from "../lib/errorText";
import { StatusChip, Spinner } from "./StatusChip";

interface Props {
  /** Called with the engine's video_id once a submitted job finishes successfully. */
  onLoaded: (videoId: string) => void;
}

export function IngestPanel({ onLoaded }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [uri, setUri] = useState("");
  const { phase, submit, cancel: cancelIngest, reset: resetIngest, busy: ingestBusy } = useIngestJob(onLoaded);
  // On upload completion, feed the returned server PATH straight into the engine processing back-half.
  const upload = useUpload((path) => submit(path));
  const busy = ingestBusy || upload.busy;

  const onSubmitUri = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    submit(uri);
  };
  const onFile = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) upload.start(file);
    e.target.value = ""; // let re-picking the same file fire change again
  };
  const cancelAll = () => {
    upload.cancel();
    cancelIngest();
  };
  const resetAll = () => {
    upload.reset();
    resetIngest();
  };

  // Shared mapping with the build flow (lib/errorText): an `engine` failure carries the engine's own
  // message (a 400 scheme/path/extension detail, a worker error); every other code maps to a friendly
  // localized line. Surface whichever half failed (upload or engine) — they're mutually exclusive.
  const errorState = upload.phase.status === "error" ? upload.phase.error : phase.status === "error" ? phase.error : null;
  const errorMsg = errorState ? errorText(t, errorState) : null;

  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-black/10 bg-white p-5" data-testid="ingest-panel">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{t("ingest.title")}</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink/60">{t("ingest.intro")}</p>

      {/* PRIMARY: upload a local file (T4). A native file input, visually a button (≥44px, A4). */}
      <div className="mt-4">
        <label
          className={
            "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-ink2 " +
            (busy ? "pointer-events-none opacity-40" : "cursor-pointer")
          }
        >
          {t("ingest.chooseFile")}
          <input
            type="file"
            accept=".mp4,.mov,video/mp4,video/quicktime"
            aria-label={t("ingest.uploadAria")}
            disabled={busy}
            onChange={onFile}
            className="sr-only"
          />
        </label>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">{t("ingest.fileHint")}</p>
      </div>

      {/* SECONDARY: or point at a cloud bucket / host path. */}
      <div className="mt-4 border-t border-black/5 pt-4">
        <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ink/40">{t("ingest.orLink")}</p>
        <form onSubmit={onSubmitUri} className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={uri}
            onChange={(e) => setUri(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={t("ingest.uriPlaceholder")}
            aria-label={t("ingest.uriAriaLabel")}
            disabled={busy}
            className="min-h-11 min-w-[18rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green disabled:opacity-50"
          />
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={busy}
            className="min-h-11 rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            {phase.status === "submitting" ? t("ingest.submitting") : t("ingest.submit")}
          </button>
        </form>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">{t("ingest.hint")}</p>
      </div>

      {/* Live status (aria-live so screen readers hear progress) as icon+text chips — never icon-only
          (A8). Upload shows a determinate % bar; the engine phase is indeterminate (phase words). */}
      <div aria-live="polite" className="mt-3 text-sm" data-testid="ingest-status">
        {upload.phase.status === "uploading" && (
          <div data-testid="upload-progress">
            <StatusChip tone="info" icon={<Spinner />} label={t("ingest.uploading", { pct: upload.phase.pct })} />
            <div
              className="mt-2 h-1.5 w-full max-w-sm overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-black/10"
              role="progressbar"
              aria-valuenow={upload.phase.pct}
              aria-valuemin={0}
              aria-valuemax={100}
            >
              <div className="h-full bg-green transition-all" style={{ width: `${upload.phase.pct}%` }} />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("ingest.reassure")}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={cancelAll}
              className="mt-2 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-xl border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green"
            >
              {t("ingest.cancel")}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        {ingestBusy && (
          <div>
            <StatusChip
              tone="info"
              icon={<Spinner />}
              label={
                t("ingest.working") +
                (phase.status === "polling" && phase.progress
                  ? ` · ${t("ingest.progress", { progress: phase.progress })}`
                  : "")
              }
            />
            {/* Reassurance for slow uplinks: the job runs on the server, so it's safe to wait. */}
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/50">{t("ingest.reassure")}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={cancelAll}
              className="mt-2 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-xl border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green"
            >
              {t("ingest.cancel")}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-red-800" data-testid="ingest-error">
            <StatusChip tone="error" icon="⚠" label={t("ingest.statusFailed")} />
            <p className="mt-2">{errorMsg}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={resetAll}
              className="mt-2 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-lg border border-red-300 px-3 text-sm hover:bg-red-100"
            >
              {t("ingest.retry")}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

3	// The ingest state machine: submit → poll (queued/running) → done | error. Drives the recursive

18	/** A fetch that answers POST /api/process with `process`, then walks `jobs` on each GET /api/jobs/*. */
19	function fetchSeq(process: unknown, jobs: JobStatus[]): FetchFn {
20	  let i = 0;

22	    const u = String(url);

24	    if (u === "/api/process" && method === "POST") return process;
25	    if (u.startsWith("/api/jobs/")) {
26	      const body = jobs[Math.min(i, jobs.length - 1)];
27	      i += 1;
28	      return httpOk(200, body);
29	    }
30	    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${method} ${u}`);
31	  }) as unknown as FetchFn;
32	}
33	
34	/** Flush the submit microtasks, then walk the backoff ladder (1000 → 1500 → 2250 → …) for n ticks. */

37	    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // flush processVideo → schedule the first poll

42	      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(delay);
43	    });
44	    delay = Math.min(delay * 1.5, 5000);
45	  }
46	}

### 283. assistant (2026-06-21T16:26:53.652Z)

// Chunked-upload client (T4): init → sequential PUT parts (byte progress) → complete, with a
// best-effort DELETE of the server-side partial on any failure. Pins the part slicing (sizes +
// strict 0,1,2 order), the progress callback, and that a mid-upload failure cleans up + rethrows.
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { uploadFile, ApiError } from "./client";

type FetchFn = typeof fetch;
const MB = 1024 * 1024;
const ok = (j: unknown) => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => j, text: async () => JSON.stringify(j) });
const httpErr = (status: number, detail: string) => ({
  ok: false, status, json: async () => ({ detail }), text: async () => JSON.stringify({ detail }),
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

/** A routing fetch over the /api/upload/* surface; records the order + sizes of the calls. */
function router(opts: { partFailsAt?: number; initStatus?: number } = {}) {
  const calls = { init: 0, parts: [] as number[], partSizes: [] as number[], complete: null as number | null, deletes: 0 };
  const f = vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const u = String(url);
    const m = init?.method ?? "GET";
    if (u === "/api/upload/init" && m === "POST") {
      calls.init += 1;
      if (opts.initStatus) return httpErr(opts.initStatus, "Extension '.txt' not allowed");
      return ok({ upload_id: "up1", max_part_mb: 1, next_part: "/api/upload/up1/part/0" });
    }
    const part = u.match(/^\/api\/upload\/up1\/part\/(\d+)$/);
    if (part && m === "PUT") {
      const idx = Number(part[1]);
      if (opts.partFailsAt === idx) return httpErr(413, "Part exceeds the per-part limit");
      const size = (init?.body as Blob).size;
      calls.parts.push(idx);
      calls.partSizes.push(size);
      return ok({ upload_id: "up1", received_parts: calls.parts.length, received_bytes: size });
    }
    if (u === "/api/upload/up1/complete" && m === "POST") {
      calls.complete = JSON.parse(init!.body as string).total_parts as number;
      return ok({ path: "/srv/uploads/match.mp4", video_id: "VID", size_bytes: 5 * MB });
    }
    if (u === "/api/upload/up1" && m === "DELETE") {
      calls.deletes += 1;
      return ok({ status: "aborted", upload_id: "up1" });
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${m} ${u}`);
  }) as unknown as FetchFn;
  return { f, calls };
}

const videoFile = (bytes: number, name = "match.mp4") =>
  new File([new Uint8Array(bytes)], name, { type: "video/mp4" });

describe("uploadFile", () => {
  it("slices into sequential parts ≤ max_part_mb, completes, and returns {path, video_id}", async () => {
    const { f, calls } = router();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    const progress: number[] = [];

    const res = await uploadFile(videoFile(2.5 * MB), { onProgress: (p) => progress.push(p.sentBytes) });

    expect(calls.parts).toEqual([0, 1, 2]); // 2.5MB / 1MB = 3 strictly-sequential parts
    expect(calls.partSizes).toEqual([1 * MB, 1 * MB, 0.5 * MB]); // last part is the remainder
    expect(calls.complete).toBe(3); // total_parts handed to complete
    expect(progress[progress.length - 1]).toBe(2.5 * MB); // progress reaches 100%
    expect(res).toMatchObject({ path: "/srv/uploads/match.mp4", video_id: "VID" });
  });

  it("a single small file uploads as ONE part", async () => {
    const { f, calls } = router();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    await uploadFile(videoFile(0.3 * MB));
    expect(calls.parts).toEqual([0]);
    expect(calls.complete).toBe(1);
  });

  it("on a part failure it DELETEs the server-side partial and rethrows the engine's reason", async () => {
    const { f, calls } = router({ partFailsAt: 1 });
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    await expect(uploadFile(videoFile(2.5 * MB))).rejects.toMatchObject({ name: "ApiError", status: 413 });
    expect(calls.parts).toEqual([0]); // stopped at the failing part
    expect(calls.deletes).toBe(1); // partial cleaned up
  });

  it("an init rejection (bad extension) rethrows and never starts parts or a cleanup", async () => {
    const { f, calls } = router({ initStatus: 400 });
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    await expect(uploadFile(videoFile(1 * MB, "notes.txt"))).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(calls.parts).toEqual([]);
    expect(calls.deletes).toBe(0); // nothing to clean — no upload_id yet
  });
});

### 286. assistant (2026-06-21T16:27:22.941Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// The upload state machine (T4): start(file) → uploading(%) → hand the server PATH to onComplete |
// error. Pins: an empty file is rejected before any network call, a happy upload calls onComplete
// with the returned path and returns to idle, and an engine rejection surfaces a classified error.
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { renderHook, act, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { useUpload } from "./useUpload";

type FetchFn = typeof fetch;
const MB = 1024 * 1024;
const ok = (j: unknown) => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => j, text: async () => JSON.stringify(j) });
const httpErr = (status: number, detail: string) => ({
  ok: false, status, json: async () => ({ detail }), text: async () => JSON.stringify({ detail }),
});

function uploadFetch(opts: { partFailsAt?: number } = {}): FetchFn {
  return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const u = String(url);
    const m = init?.method ?? "GET";
    if (u === "/api/upload/init") return ok({ upload_id: "up1", max_part_mb: 1, next_part: "/api/upload/up1/part/0" });
    const part = u.match(/\/part\/(\d+)$/);
    if (part && m === "PUT") {
      if (opts.partFailsAt === Number(part[1])) return httpErr(413, "Part exceeds the per-part limit");
      return ok({ upload_id: "up1", received_parts: 1, received_bytes: (init?.body as Blob).size });
    }
    if (u.endsWith("/complete")) return ok({ path: "/srv/uploads/match.mp4", video_id: "VID", size_bytes: 1 });
    if (m === "DELETE") return ok({ status: "aborted" });
    throw new Error(`unexpected ${m} ${u}`);
  }) as unknown as FetchFn;
}

const videoFile = (bytes: number, name = "match.mp4") => new File([new Uint8Array(bytes)], name, { type: "video/mp4" });

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe("useUpload", () => {
  it("rejects an empty file before any network call", () => {
    const f = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f as unknown as FetchFn);
    const onComplete = vi.fn();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useUpload(onComplete));

    act(() => result.current.start(videoFile(0)));

    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalidUri" } });
    expect(f).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(onComplete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("uploads a file and hands the returned server path to onComplete", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", uploadFetch());
    const onComplete = vi.fn();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useUpload(onComplete));

    act(() => result.current.start(videoFile(0.3 * MB)));
    await waitFor(() => expect(onComplete).toHaveBeenCalledWith("/srv/uploads/match.mp4"));
    expect(result.current.phase.status).toBe("idle"); // handed off — the engine phase takes over
  });

  it("surfaces a classified error when a part is rejected", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", uploadFetch({ partFailsAt: 0 }));
    const onComplete = vi.fn();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useUpload(onComplete));

    act(() => result.current.start(videoFile(0.3 * MB)));
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.phase.status).toBe("error"));
    expect(onComplete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    // a 413 with a {detail} body classifies as an engine error (its message survives)
    expect(result.current.phase).toMatchObject({ status: "error", error: { code: "engine" } });
  });
});

### 289. assistant (2026-06-21T16:28:23.244Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// T4 end to end through IngestPanel: pick a local file → it chunks up to /api/upload/* → the returned
// server PATH is fed to POST /api/process → poll → onLoaded(video_id). The keystone for alpha users
// who have a FILE, not a gs:// URI. Also pins that the file picker is present + labelled (a11y/A4).
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent, act, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import { IngestPanel } from "./IngestPanel";

const MB = 1024 * 1024;
const ok = (j: unknown) => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => j, text: async () => JSON.stringify(j) });

let processBody: { video_path?: string } | null = null;

function uploadThenProcessFetch() {
  processBody = null;
  return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const u = String(url);
    const m = init?.method ?? "GET";
    if (u === "/api/upload/init") return ok({ upload_id: "up1", max_part_mb: 1, next_part: "/api/upload/up1/part/0" });
    if (u.includes("/api/upload/up1/part/")) return ok({ upload_id: "up1", received_parts: 1, received_bytes: (init?.body as Blob).size });
    if (u.endsWith("/complete")) return ok({ path: "/srv/uploads/match.mp4", video_id: "PRE", size_bytes: 1 });
    if (u === "/api/process" && m === "POST") {
      processBody = JSON.parse(init!.body as string);
      return ok({ job_id: "j", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j" });
    }
    if (u.startsWith("/api/jobs/")) return ok({ job_id: "j", status: "done", video_id: "VIDFINAL", result: { status: "success", video_id: "VIDFINAL" } });
    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${m} ${u}`);
  }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
}

const videoFile = (bytes: number) => new File([new Uint8Array(bytes)], "match.mp4", { type: "video/mp4" });

const pickFile = (file: File) => {
  const input = screen.getByLabelText("Choose a video file to upload");
  Object.defineProperty(input, "files", { value: [file], configurable: true });
  fireEvent.change(input);
};

beforeEach(async () => {
  window.localStorage.clear();
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
});

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("T4: in-UI file upload", () => {
  it("offers a labelled file picker (present for a keyboard/SR user)", () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn());
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Choose a video file to upload")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Choose a video file")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("uploads the picked file, feeds the server path to /api/process, and loads the resulting video_id", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", uploadThenProcessFetch());
    const onLoaded = vi.fn();
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={onLoaded} />);

    pickFile(videoFile(0.3 * MB));

    // Flush the upload chain (init → part → complete) + the process submit → schedules the poll.
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    });
    // The uploaded server PATH (not the URI) is what gets processed.
    await waitFor(() => expect(processBody?.video_path).toBe("/srv/uploads/match.mp4"));

    // Poll tick → done → the job's canonical video_id is handed up.
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000);
    });
    expect(onLoaded).toHaveBeenCalledWith("VIDFINAL");
  });
});

diff --git a/web/src/api/client.ts b/web/src/api/client.ts
index f59884e..92cb9fb 100644
--- a/web/src/api/client.ts
+++ b/web/src/api/client.ts
@@ -2,7 +2,17 @@
 // UI + engine behind one origin; in dev Vite proxies `/api` → :8000). Every call carries a
 // correlation id (header + logs) so a journey is traceable end to end.
 import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
-import type { RallySegment, QueryResponse, CompileResponse, EngineConfig, ProcessResponse, JobStatus } from "./types";
+import type {
+  RallySegment,
+  QueryResponse,
+  CompileResponse,
+  EngineConfig,
+  ProcessResponse,
+  JobStatus,
+  UploadInitResponse,
+  UploadPartResponse,
+  UploadCompleteResponse,
+} from "./types";
 
 const BASE = "/api";
 
@@ -112,3 +122,98 @@ export async function getConfig(cid?: string): Promise<EngineConfig> {
 export function minShotCount(cfg: EngineConfig): number | undefined {
   return cfg.stitching?.min_shot_count;
 }
+
+// ── chunked upload (backend/api/uploads.py) ──────────────────────────────────
+// A browser uploads a LOCAL file in sequential parts ≤ max_part_mb (free-tier edge proxies cap
+// bodies ~100 MB), then `complete` returns a server-host `path` to feed straight into /api/process.
+
+/** Start a chunked upload. 400 = bad extension; 413 = over the size limit [uploads.py:64]. */
+export async function uploadInit(filename: string, totalSizeBytes: number, cid?: string): Promise<UploadInitResponse> {
+  return request<UploadInitResponse>("/upload/init", {
+    method: "POST",
+    body: JSON.stringify({ filename, total_size_bytes: totalSizeBytes }),
+    cid,
+  });
+}
+
+/** Append ONE part (raw bytes — not JSON). Parts are strictly sequential 0,1,2,… [uploads.py:89]. */
+export async function uploadPart(
+  uploadId: string,
+  index: number,
+  chunk: Blob,
+  cid?: string,
+  signal?: AbortSignal,
+): Promise<UploadPartResponse> {
+  const c = cid ?? newCorrelationId();
+  const url = `${BASE}/upload/${encodeURIComponent(uploadId)}/part/${index}`;
+  log("info", "api.request", { cid: c, method: "PUT", url });
+  let res: Response;
+  try {
+    res = await fetch(url, {
+      method: "PUT",
+      body: chunk,
+      headers: { "content-type": "application/octet-stream", "x-correlation-id": c },
+      signal,
+    });
+  } catch (err) {
+    log("error", "api.network_error", { cid: c, method: "PUT", url, error: String(err) });
+    throw new ApiError(`Network error calling PUT ${url}`, { cid: c, cause: err });
+  }
+  if (!res.ok) {
+    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
+    log("error", "api.http_error", { cid: c, method: "PUT", url, status: res.status, body: body.slice(0, 500) });
+    throw new ApiError(`PUT ${url} failed: ${res.status}`, { cid: c, status: res.status, body });
+  }
+  log("info", "api.response", { cid: c, method: "PUT", url, status: res.status });
+  return (await res.json()) as UploadPartResponse;
+}
+
+/** Finalize: returns {path, video_id} — feed `path` to processVideo() [uploads.py:123]. */
+export async function uploadComplete(uploadId: string, totalParts: number, cid?: string): Promise<UploadCompleteResponse> {
+  return request<UploadCompleteResponse>(`/upload/${encodeURIComponent(uploadId)}/complete`, {
+    method: "POST",
+    body: JSON.stringify({ total_parts: totalParts }),
+    cid,
+  });
+}
+
+/** Abort an in-flight upload (best-effort cleanup of the server-side partial) [uploads.py:147]. */
+export async function uploadAbort(uploadId: string, cid?: string): Promise<void> {
+  await request<unknown>(`/upload/${encodeURIComponent(uploadId)}`, { method: "DELETE", cid });
+}
+
+export interface UploadProgress {
+  sentBytes: number;
+  totalBytes: number;
+}
+
+/** Upload a local File end to end: init → sequential parts (byte progress, abortable) → complete.
+ *  On ANY failure or abort it best-effort DELETEs the server-side partial, then rethrows so the
+ *  caller can classify it (the engine's 400/413 detail survives). Returns {path, video_id}. */
+export async function uploadFile(
+  file: File,
+  opts: { cid?: string; signal?: AbortSignal; onProgress?: (p: UploadProgress) => void } = {},
+): Promise<UploadCompleteResponse> {
+  const cid = opts.cid ?? newCorrelationId();
+  const init = await uploadInit(file.name, file.size, cid);
+  const partBytes = Math.max(1, init.max_part_mb) * 1024 * 1024;
+  const totalParts = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(file.size / partBytes));
+  log("info", "upload.start", { cid, filename: file.name, size: file.size, parts: totalParts, part_mb: init.max_part_mb });
+  let sent = 0;
+  try {
+    for (let i = 0; i < totalParts; i++) {
+      if (opts.signal?.aborted) throw new DOMException("Upload aborted", "AbortError");
+      const chunk = file.slice(i * partBytes, Math.min((i + 1) * partBytes, file.size));
+      await uploadPart(init.upload_id, i, chunk, cid, opts.signal);
+      sent += chunk.size;
+      opts.onProgress?.({ sentBytes: sent, totalBytes: file.size });
+    }
+    const done = await uploadComplete(init.upload_id, totalParts, cid);
+    log("info", "upload.done", { cid, video_id: done.video_id, path: done.path, size: done.size_bytes });
+    return done;
+  } catch (err) {
+    log("warn", "upload.aborting_partial", { cid, upload_id: init.upload_id, error: String(err) });
+    await uploadAbort(init.upload_id, cid).catch(() => {});
+    throw err;
+  }
+}
