### 367. user (2026-06-21T16:54:14.169Z)

1	# Multilingual Expansion — 7 new locales (th · id · da · es · zh-Hans · ja · ko)
2	
3	> **Status:** `DRAFT` — **owner-gated (approval needed to START this line)** · **Owner:** Avi Dullu · **Created:** 2026-06-21 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-21
4	> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE)
5	> **Tracking anchors:** §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; index in `docs/README.md`; pointer in memory `current-state`; thread in `NEXT_STEPS.md` once work starts.
6	> **Relation to existing docs:** **PEER-OF / extends** [`FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md`](FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md) (the `en`/`kn`/`hi` foundation) and the engine design `I18N_PLAN.md`. **Reuses that foundation verbatim** (react-i18next + i18next-icu + browser detector + key-parity/no-bare-strings CI + self-hosted OFL Noto via `@fontsource` + Gemini-draft→native-review). This is a **parallel** line: it adds locales, it does **not** re-architect i18n.
7	> **Honesty note:** each claim tagged `[verified]` (checked vs the working tree this session), `[researched]` (sourced, needs sign-off), or `[design]` (proposed). Not legal advice — the OFL-font carve-out is the owner's standing call (D7 in FRIENDLY).
8	
9	---
10	
11	## 0. TL;DR
12	Add **Thai, Indonesian, Danish, Spanish, Chinese (Simplified), Japanese, Korean** to the Studio SPA with the **same rigor as en/kn/hi**. The good news `[verified]`: the i18n foundation already scales — **zero changes to the negotiation contract or the catalog format**; adding a locale is "drop a reviewed `common.json` + (if non-Latin) bundle one OFL Noto font + keep CI key-parity green." The work splits cleanly into **Phase A** (es/da/id — *catalog-only, no fonts*), **Phase B** (Thai — *one small OFL font*), and **Phase C** (zh-Hans/ja/ko — *large OFL CJK fonts + lazy-load + an offline-bundle subsetting decision + the zh script-variant call*). **The single most important caveats:** (1) **native-speaker review per language** is the real cost and the public-launch gate (Gemini drafts are *inference-only*, never the source of truth — the standing guardrail); (2) **CJK fonts add tens of MB** to the otherwise-tiny air-gapped bundle; (3) **Spanish now has a 3-form plural** (`{one, many, other}`) — the one authoring wrinkle.
13	
14	## 1. Problem & goal
15	**Why now.** en/kn/hi shipped and proved the foundation is locale-pluggable (a Kannada-default browser auto-renders Kannada; the switcher walks kn→hi→en). The owner wants to broaden reach beyond the India beachhead — **SE Asia (th, id), Europe (da, es), and East Asia (zh, ja, ko)**.
16	**Concrete outcome ("good"):** a user whose browser is in any of the 7 languages lands on the Studio (and, once `site/` is localized via FRIENDLY B3, the marketing pages too), and — without help — understands the product, runs the tool, and gets a reel, **entirely in their language**, with the fonts rendering crisply offline.
17	**Read to get current:** this doc §3–§4; [`FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md`](FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md) (the foundation this rides on, esp. §4 design + §6.5 negotiation tests); `web/src/i18n/index.ts` (the detector/ICU/fallback config) + `web/src/main.tsx` (the `@fontsource` font-load pattern) + `web/src/i18n/hygiene.test.ts` (key-parity + no-bare-strings + offline guards).
18	
19	## 2. Decisions locked (inherited from FRIENDLY unless noted)
20	| # | Decision | Source / date | Implication |
21	|---|----------|---------------|-------------|
22	| ED1 | **Same framework, no re-architecture** — react-i18next + i18next-icu + `i18next-browser-languagedetector`; one `en` source catalog + per-locale siblings; **always fall back to `en`** | FRIENDLY D-* / `i18n/index.ts` `[verified]` | Each new locale = add to `SUPPORTED_LOCALES` + a `common.json`; negotiation/format unchanged |
23	| ED2 | **Self-host OFL Noto via `@fontsource`** (the D7 OFL-for-media carve-out) — **extend it to the 4 new Noto fonts** (Thai, SC, JP, KR) | FRIENDLY D7 (owner, 2026-06-21) | No new licensing decision; same `main.tsx` import + `unicode-range`-scoped pattern as kn/hi |
24	| ED3 | **Gemini-draft → native-speaker review** (inference only, never training; every value flagged for review) | `CLAUDE.md` guardrail | Translation *quality* is human-gated + owner-paced; CI proves parity, not quality |
25	| ED4 | **CI key-parity + no-bare-strings stay merge gates** for every locale | `hygiene.test.ts` `[verified]` | A new catalog must match the `en` key set exactly or CI fails |
26	| ED5 | **All 7 are LTR** — no RTL/bidi layout work in this batch | `[researched]` CLDR/W3C | No `dir="rtl"`, no logical-property audit; RTL (ar/he/ur) would be a separate, larger track |
27	| ED6 *(proposed)* | **Chinese = `zh-Hans` (Simplified) for v1**; `zh-Hant` (Traditional) is a *separate* catalog + font, added later only on demand | §8 Q2 `[design]` | Key the catalog `zh-Hans` (not bare `zh`) so `zh-Hant` slots in cleanly |
28	
29	## 3. Foundation — research / prior art `[researched]`
30	Per-language diligence (Unicode CLDR plural-rules chart; SIL OFL; `@fontsource`; W3C BCP-47 — sources in §10):
31	
32	| Lang | BCP-47 | CLDR **cardinal** plurals | Dir | Dedicated font (OFL) | `@fontsource(-variable)` pkg | Font size / notes |
33	|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
34	| Thai | `th` | **`other`** (1 form) | LTR | **Noto Sans Thai** | `noto-sans-thai` | **small** (single script block) · no-space word-wrap gotcha |
35	| Indonesian | `id` | **`other`** (1 form) | LTR | — (Latin) | — | pure catalog add |
36	| Danish | `da` | **`one, other`** (2) | LTR | — (Latin) | — | same shape as English (`æ ø å` in Latin font) |
37	| Spanish | `es` | **`one, many, other`** (3) ⚠ | LTR | — (Latin) | — | `many` = millions/compact only; ~15–30% string expansion |
38	| Chinese (Simpl.) | **`zh-Hans`** | **`other`** (1 form) | LTR | **Noto Sans SC** | `noto-sans-sc` | **very large** (~7–16 MB/weight unsubset) · script-variant decision |
39	| Japanese | `ja` | **`other`** (1 form) | LTR | **Noto Sans JP** | `noto-sans-jp` | **very large** (CJK) |
40	| Korean | `ko` | **`other`** (1 form) | LTR | **Noto Sans KR** | `noto-sans-kr` | **very large** (Hangul + Han) |
41	
42	**Three findings that drive the design:**
43	1. **Spanish's 3-form plural** is the only authoring change. Current CLDR `es` cardinal = `{one, many, other}`; `many` fires only on large/compact numbers (e.g. millions), but every `es` ICU plural message must still carry a `many {…}` arm or `intl-messageformat` falls back. Flag it to the reviewer and extend the parity/lint check. (th/id/zh/ja/ko are **single-form** → trivial; da is 2-form like en.)
44	2. **Fonts split the batch.** es/da/id need **no new font** (Latin). th needs one **small** OFL font. **zh-Hans/ja/ko need large OFL CJK fonts** — all OFL-1.1 (bundleable under ED2), all on `@fontsource-variable`, but multi-MB each.
45	3. **CJK + the offline guard collide.** `@fontsource` serves CJK as ~100+ `unicode-range` `woff2` chunks, fetched on demand — efficient *online*, but the SPA's **air-gapped/offline guarantee** (`hygiene.test.ts` "offline / self-contained") means we must ship **all** chunks so any glyph renders with no network → **tens of MB** added with 3 CJK languages. Mitigations in §4.
46	
47	## 4. Design / architecture
48	**Reuse map (what's reused vs new):**
49	- *Reused unchanged:* the negotiation contract (persisted pref → `?lang=` → navigator → `en`), the ICU catalog format, the `@fontsource` `unicode-range`-scoped self-host pattern (`main.tsx:7-8`), the key-parity + no-bare-strings + offline CI (`hygiene.test.ts`), the draft→review pipeline, the `<html lang>` sync (`i18n/index.ts:59-65`).
50	- *New:* 7 `common.json` catalogs; `SUPPORTED_LOCALES` entries (`i18n/index.ts:18`); **4 new Noto font imports**; an **es 3-form plural** allowance in the catalog + tooling; a **CJK lazy-load + offline-subset** strategy (Phase C); Thai line-break CSS safety net.
51	- **⚠ Catalog-home caveat `[verified]`:** catalogs today live at `web/src/i18n/locales/{en,kn,hi}/common.json` — **not** a shared top-level `locales/` (the I18N_PLAN "D-CATALOG-HOME" diagram). This expansion adds siblings there. If FRIENDLY **B3** (the shared `site/` `t()` shim) hoists them to a shared dir first, these new catalogs move with them — sequence-aware, not a blocker.
52	
53	**Negotiation tests (per FRIENDLY §6.5):** every new locale gets an N1/N2-style test — a browser set to that language **auto-renders** it (e.g. `navigator.languages=["ja-JP"]` → `ja`), region subtags normalize (`zh-Hans-CN` → `zh-Hans`), and a missing key falls back to `en`. **Auto-selection is the promise; it's proven by a test, never assumed.**
54	
55	**Phasing (least new code/assets first):**
56	- **Phase A — Latin trio (`es`, `da`, `id`): catalog-only, zero new assets.** Highest ROI, proves the pipeline scales beyond Indic with no code. Only `es` needs the 3-form-plural touch.
57	- **Phase B — Thai (`th`): one small OFL font.** Reuses the kn/hi font pattern exactly; add the no-space word-wrap CSS safety net. Self-contained, low-risk.
58	- **Phase C — CJK (`zh-Hans`, `ja`, `ko`): the heavy slice.** Needs the zh-Hans/Hant decision, 3 large OFL fonts, **per-locale lazy font-load** (so non-CJK users never download CJK), and an **offline-bundle subset strategy** (ship-all-chunks vs a custom subset build step vs online-lazy-only). One PR per CJK language.
59	
60	## 5. Privacy / scope note
61	i18n is **presentation-only** — no new personal-data collection, no biometrics, minors-excluded posture unchanged (same as FRIENDLY §5). Gemini-draft translation is inference, not training, with no user data in the loop. CJK/Thai font bundling is OFL media, not code/weights.
62	
63	## 6. Honest limits — what this does NOT do
64	- A green CI / DONE proves **key-parity + render + no-bare-strings + auto-negotiation**, **not** translation *quality* (native-review-gated, ED3) or correct font rendering (owner-eyeball-gated, like kn/hi).
65	- **`zh-Hant` (Traditional) is out of v1** (separate catalog + font); only `zh-Hans` ships first.
66	- **No RTL** — this batch is all LTR.
67	- The **CJK offline bundle is large** (tens of MB) unless a subset build is adopted — a real size trade-off, not free.
68	- `site/` marketing localization for these 7 **depends on FRIENDLY B3** landing first (the shared shim); until then this is SPA-only.
69	
70	## 7. Deliverables & progress tracker   ⟵ **source of truth**
71	Legend: ☐ Todo · ◐ In progress · ☑ Done · ⛔ Blocked/gated. **One small PR per row**, branched from `origin/master`, no stacking.
72	
73	| ID | Deliverable | Phase | Depends on | Gated? | Status | PR |
74	|----|-------------|-------|-----------|--------|--------|----|
75	| E0 | This proposal doc + inbound pointers (README/NEXT_STEPS/memory) | — | — | **Owner approval to start** | ☐ | — |
76	| EA1 | **Spanish (`es`)** catalog (Gemini-draft) + ICU **3-form `{one,many,other}`** handling + extend key-parity/no-bare-strings tooling for the `many` arm + negotiation test | A | E0 | No (after approval) | ☐ | — |
77	| EA2 | **Danish (`da`)** catalog + negotiation test | A | E0 | No | ☐ | — |
78	| EA3 | **Indonesian (`id`)** catalog + negotiation test | A | E0 | No | ☐ | — |
79	| EB1 | **Noto Sans Thai** bundle (OFL, `@fontsource-variable`) + Thai no-space word-wrap CSS safety net | B | E0 | No (ED2 carve-out applies) | ☐ | — |
80	| EB2 | **Thai (`th`)** catalog → render + negotiation test | B | EB1 | No | ☐ | — |
81	| EC0 | **Decision + design:** `zh-Hans` vs `zh-Hant` (ED6) + CJK **lazy per-locale font-load** + **offline-bundle subset** strategy (ship-all-chunks vs custom subset vs online-lazy) | C | E0 | **Owner (Q2/Q3)** | ☐ | — |
82	| EC1 | **Noto Sans SC** + **`zh-Hans`** catalog + lazy-load + negotiation test | C | EC0 | No (after EC0) | ☐ | — |
83	| EC2 | **Noto Sans JP** + **`ja`** catalog + lazy-load + negotiation test | C | EC0 | No | ☐ | — |
84	| EC3 | **Noto Sans KR** + **`ko`** catalog + lazy-load + negotiation test | C | EC0 | No | ☐ | — |
85	| EX1 | **Native-speaker review** of each AI-drafted catalog (one tracked row; per-language sign-off) | — | the catalog rows | **Owner (reviewer sourcing)** | ☐ | — |
86	| EX2 | **Owner-machine font eyeball** per new script (th, zh, ja, ko) — cloud CI can't render fonts | — | EB*/EC* | **Owner (owner machine)** | ☐ | — |
87	| EX3 | **`site/` marketing parity** for the 7 (rides FRIENDLY **B3** shared `t()` shim) | — | FRIENDLY B3 | Blocked on B3 | ☐ | — |
88	
89	## 8. Open questions — owner
90	1. **Approve starting this line** (in parallel with the FRIENDLY UX/A-items), and the **A → B → C phasing**? *(E0 gate.)*
91	2. **Chinese:** ship **`zh-Hans` only** for v1 (recommended), or **both Hans + Hant**? *(EC0; Hant = a whole extra catalog + Noto Sans TC.)*
92	3. **CJK offline strategy** *(EC0; the real size trade-off):* (a) ship **all** font chunks → simplest, **+tens of MB** to the air-gapped bundle; (b) a **custom subset build** restricted to glyphs the reviewed catalogs use → KB-scale but adds a build step that re-runs on catalog change; (c) **online lazy-load only** → small bundle but **breaks the offline guarantee for CJK**. *Recommend (b) if offline-for-CJK matters, else (a).*
93	4. **Native-reviewer sourcing** for 7 languages — who/how/budget? (Vendor like the Roora labeling pilot? community? paid freelancers?) This is the launch-gate cost.
94	5. **Priority order** — which markets first? (Default A-then-B-then-C is by *engineering cost*; if a market is urgent, e.g. `id` or `ja`, it can jump within its phase.)
95	6. **Does `site/` localize for these too** for v1, or SPA-only until FRIENDLY B3 lands? *(EX3.)*
96	
97	## 9. Definition of done
98	- [ ] All 7 catalogs (`es`, `da`, `id`, `th`, `zh-Hans`, `ja`, `ko`) live + **rendering** on the `web/` SPA; switcher + persistence + `en` fallback; **no raw keys**.
99	- [ ] **Auto-negotiation green** — a browser set to each language auto-renders it (N1/N2-style tests per locale); region subtags normalize; missing-key → `en`.
100	- [ ] **CI key-parity + no-bare-strings green** (incl. the `es` 3-form plural) + the **offline/self-contained guard** still green after the CJK fonts land.
101	- [ ] **Fonts render correctly** — owner eyeball per new script (th, zh-Hans, ja, ko).
102	- [ ] **Native-speaker review** complete + signed off per language (EX1).
103	- [ ] CJK **offline/bundle-size** strategy implemented per the EC0 decision; documented bundle-size impact.
104	- [ ] *(if in scope)* `site/` marketing renders the 7 via the shared shim (EX3, after FRIENDLY B3).
105	- [ ] §7 all rows ☑ (or explicitly deferred) → flip **Status → DONE** + archive.
106	
107	## 10. References
108	**Internal `[verified]`:** `web/src/i18n/{index.ts,hygiene.test.ts}`, `web/src/main.tsx` (font-load pattern), `web/src/i18n/locales/{en,kn,hi}/common.json`; [`FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md`](FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md), engine `docs/I18N_PLAN.md`, `docs/UI_PROPOSAL.md`.
109	**External `[researched]`:** Unicode CLDR v48 plural-rules chart (https://www.unicode.org/cldr/charts/48/supplemental/language_plural_rules.html) + plural-rules spec; W3C BCP-47 / Chinese script subtags (`zh-Hans`/`zh-Hant`); SIL Open Font License 1.1 + Noto CJK LICENSE (github.com/notofonts/noto-cjk); `@fontsource-variable/noto-sans-{thai,sc,jp,kr}`; W3C Thai line-breaking (`thai-gap`); react-i18next + i18next-icu.

### 437. assistant (2026-06-21T17:12:45.925Z)

// Localized tap-only filter controls (tracker A9). The query bar PARSES ENGLISH text (a documented
// limit) — so a Kannada/Hindi/… user who can't type English needs to filter by TAPPING. Each control
// builds the SAME ParsedQuery the parser would for its English equivalent — directly, no English
// round-trip — and applies it through the shared onApply path. A test pins tap == type so a control
// can never silently drift from the parser. Length-led + fully-available only (never shot-count).
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import type { ParsedQuery } from "../lib/query";

interface TapFilter {
  /** i18n label key (localized) — the user sees this, not the English query. */
  key: string;
  /** The equivalent typed query; the tap MUST select identically to typing this (pinned by a test). */
  english: string;
  parts: Pick<ParsedQuery, "filters" | "sort" | "limit">;
}

export const TAP_FILTERS: TapFilter[] = [
  { key: "tap.longest", english: "longest first", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" } } },
  { key: "tap.shortest", english: "shortest first", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "asc" } } },
  { key: "tap.over10", english: "over 10s", parts: { filters: [{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 }] } },
  { key: "tap.over20", english: "over 20s", parts: { filters: [{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 20 }] } },
  { key: "tap.top5", english: "top 5", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }, limit: 5 } },
  { key: "tap.top10", english: "top 10", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }, limit: 10 } },
];

interface Props {
  /** Same handler the QueryBar uses — App runs runQuery + writes the shared selection. */
  onApply: (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => void;
}

export function TapFilters({ onApply }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  // ≥44px tap targets (A4) so the no-type path is comfortable on a phone.
  const BTN = "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green";

  return (
    <div className="mt-3" data-testid="tap-filters">
      <span className="text-xs text-ink/40">{t("tap.title")}</span>
      <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {TAP_FILTERS.map((f) => (
          <button
            key={f.key}
            type="button"
            className={BTN}
            onClick={() =>
              onApply(
                { raw: `tap:${f.key}`, filters: f.parts.filters, sort: f.parts.sort, limit: f.parts.limit, unavailable: [], recognized: true },
                "example",
              )
            }
          >
            {t(f.key)}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

### 451. assistant (2026-06-21T17:14:51.302Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// A9 — localized tap-only filters. The load-bearing guarantee: each tap selects IDENTICALLY to
// typing its English equivalent (tap == type), so a Kannada/Hindi/… user who taps gets exactly what
// an English user who types gets — the parser stays the single source of truth.
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n";
import { TapFilters, TAP_FILTERS } from "./TapFilters";
import { parseQuery } from "../lib/query";

beforeEach(async () => {
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
});
afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe("TapFilters (A9)", () => {
  it("each tap builds the SAME query as typing its English equivalent (tap == type)", () => {
    for (const f of TAP_FILTERS) {
      const typed = parseQuery(f.english);
      expect(typed.recognized, f.english).toBe(true);
      expect(typed.unavailable, f.english).toEqual([]); // length-led, fully available — never shot-count
      expect({ filters: typed.filters, sort: typed.sort, limit: typed.limit }, f.english).toEqual({
        filters: f.parts.filters,
        sort: f.parts.sort,
        limit: f.parts.limit,
      });
    }
  });

  it("tapping a control applies a recognized query through onApply", () => {
    const onApply = vi.fn();
    render(<TapFilters onApply={onApply} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Over 10 seconds" }));
    expect(onApply).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const [q, source] = onApply.mock.calls[0];
    expect(source).toBe("example");
    expect(q.recognized).toBe(true);
    expect(q.filters).toEqual([{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 }]);
    expect(q.unavailable).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("renders localized control labels (Kannada)", async () => {
    await i18n.changeLanguage("kn");
    render(<TapFilters onApply={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.getByText("ಅತಿ ಉದ್ದದವು ಮೊದಲು")).toBeTruthy(); // tap.longest → Kannada
    await i18n.changeLanguage("en");
  });

  it("every control is a ≥44px tap target (A4)", () => {
    render(<TapFilters onApply={() => {}} />);
    for (const f of TAP_FILTERS) {
      expect(screen.getByText(i18n.t(f.key)).className, f.key).toMatch(/min-h-11/);
    }
  });
});

+// Localized tap-only filter controls (tracker A9). The query bar PARSES ENGLISH text (a documented
+// limit) — so a Kannada/Hindi/… user who can't type English needs to filter by TAPPING. Each control
+// builds the SAME ParsedQuery the parser would for its English equivalent — directly, no English
+// round-trip — and applies it through the shared onApply path. A test pins tap == type so a control
+// can never silently drift from the parser. Length-led + fully-available only (never shot-count).
+import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
+import type { ParsedQuery } from "../lib/query";
+
+interface TapFilter {
+  /** i18n label key (localized) — the user sees this, not the English query. */
+  key: string;
+  /** The equivalent typed query; the tap MUST select identically to typing this (pinned by a test). */
+  english: string;
+  parts: Pick<ParsedQuery, "filters" | "sort" | "limit">;
+}
+
+export const TAP_FILTERS: TapFilter[] = [
+  { key: "tap.longest", english: "longest first", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" } } },
+  { key: "tap.shortest", english: "shortest first", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "asc" } } },
+  { key: "tap.over10", english: "over 10s", parts: { filters: [{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 10 }] } },
+  { key: "tap.over20", english: "over 20s", parts: { filters: [{ field: "duration", op: ">", value: 20 }] } },
+  { key: "tap.top5", english: "top 5", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }, limit: 5 } },
+  { key: "tap.top10", english: "top 10", parts: { filters: [], sort: { key: "duration", dir: "desc" }, limit: 10 } },
+];
+
+interface Props {
+  /** Same handler the QueryBar uses — App runs runQuery + writes the shared selection. */
+  onApply: (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => void;
+}
+
+export function TapFilters({ onApply }: Props) {
+  const { t } = useTranslation();
+  // ≥44px tap targets (A4) so the no-type path is comfortable on a phone.
+  const BTN = "inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded-full border border-black/15 px-4 text-sm hover:border-green";
+
+  return (
+    <div className="mt-3" data-testid="tap-filters">
+      <span className="text-xs text-ink/40">{t("tap.title")}</span>
+      <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
+        {TAP_FILTERS.map((f) => (
+          <button
+            key={f.key}
+            type="button"
+            className={BTN}
+            onClick={() =>
+              onApply(
+                { raw: `tap:${f.key}`, filters: f.parts.filters, sort: f.parts.sort, limit: f.parts.limit, unavailable: [], recognized: true },
+                "example",
+              )
+            }
+          >
+            {t(f.key)}
+          </button>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

### 531. assistant (2026-06-21T17:32:52.298Z)

Verified against the schema and the API — answer is **no**.

The site's rendered locale is **purely a browser-side concern** and never reaches the engine or the database:

**What actually gets stored when a video is processed:**
- `videos` table ([database.py:118](backend/infrastructure/database.py:118)): `id, filepath, processed_at, status, validation_results` (+ `user_id` from the v4 migration). No locale.
- `jobs` table ([database.py:193](backend/infrastructure/database.py:193), one row per `/api/process`): `id, video_path, video_id, segmenter, player_pool, max_frames, status, progress, error, result, created_at/started_at/finished_at` (+ `policy`, `next_poll_at`, and the v6 cost columns). No locale.
- `play_segments` / `events` / `candidate_segments` — no locale either.

**Where the locale lives instead:** only in the browser — the i18n detector persists the choice to `localStorage["khelsutra.lang"]` ([web/src/i18n/index.ts:24](web/src/i18n/index.ts:24)). The SPA's outgoing calls don't carry it: `processVideo` sends only `{video_path}` and `uploadInit` sends only `{filename, total_size_bytes}` ([web/src/api/client.ts](web/src/api/client.ts)). The engine does no `Accept-Language` handling. (The only `language` hit in the backend is the `obsreport` config-DSL in `reporting/spec.yaml` — unrelated, and the dead `backend/api/static/`.)

So today a Kannada-rendered session and an English-rendered session that process the same file produce identical DB rows.

**Whether that's a gap depends on what you want it for** — and there are two real reasons it might matter:
1. **Localized output** — a Kannada/Hindi **reel watermark** or a localized report. The engine's `WatermarkConfig.font_path` already takes a path (FRIENDLY §4 flagged this), but the engine would need to *know* the locale at process time to pick the language/font. Recording it is the natural way.
2. **Analytics** — which language markets are actually using the tool (a PMF signal).

If you want it, it's a small, clean, additive change: add an optional `locale` to the `/api/process` (and upload-init) request, have the SPA pass `i18n.resolvedLanguage`, and store it as a nullable column on `videos` (or `jobs`) via a v7 migration — additive-NULL, default-OFF behavior, no impact on existing rows.

Want me to spec/build that (it'd touch the engine, so I'd coordinate with the concurrent serving session and likely fold it into the same v7 migration they're planning), or were you just checking the current state?

16	class VideoCompiler:

18	                 watermark: Optional[Any] = None):

27	    @staticmethod
28	    def _normalize_watermark(watermark: Optional[Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
29	        if watermark is None:
30	            return None
31	        if hasattr(watermark, "model_dump"):
32	            return watermark.model_dump()
33	        if isinstance(watermark, dict):

36	
37	    @staticmethod
38	    def _ff_quote(path: str) -> str:
39	        """Make a filesystem path safe to embed inside single quotes in an ffmpeg
40	        filtergraph operand (drawtext fontfile/textfile, movie= source): forward
41	        slashes (Windows-friendly), escaped single quotes, AND escaped colons.
42	
43	        The colon MUST be escaped even inside single quotes: ffmpeg's filtergraph
44	        option parser treats ':' as the option separator, so a Windows drive-letter
45	        path like ``C:/dir/font.ttf`` otherwise truncates the operand at ``C`` and
46	        the encode fails with "Invalid argument". This silently disabled the
47	        on-by-default bundled-font watermark (and absolute logo paths) on Windows."""
48	        return path.replace("\\", "/").replace("'", r"\'").replace(":", r"\:")
49	
50	    @staticmethod
51	    def _parse_time(val: Union[int, float, str]) -> float:
52	        """Normalizes a timestamp value to float seconds.
53	        Handles: float/int (pass-through), plain numeric strings ("12.4"),
54	        and colon-separated strings ("MM:SS", "HH:MM:SS", "H:MM:SS.ms").
55	        This mirrors the parse_time logic used in gemini.py to ensure
56	        the stitcher can always consume whatever format the indexer produced."""
57	        if isinstance(val, (int, float)):
58	            return float(val)
59	        if isinstance(val, str):
60	            val = val.strip()
61	            if ":" in val:
62	                # Handle MM:SS, HH:MM:SS, etc.
63	                parts = val.split(":")
64	                parts.reverse()
65	                total = 0.0
66	                for i, p in enumerate(parts):
67	                    try:
68	                        total += float(p) * (60 ** i)
69	                    except ValueError:
70	                        pass
71	                return total
72	            try:
73	                return float(val)
74	            except ValueError:
75	                logger.warning(f"Could not parse timestamp value '{val}' as a number. Defaulting to 0.0.")
76	                return 0.0
77	        logger.warning(f"Unexpected timestamp type {type(val).__name__} for value '{val}'. Defaulting to 0.0.")
78	        return 0.0
79	
80	    @staticmethod
81	    def _merge_overlapping(segments: List[Tuple[float, float]]) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:
82	        """Normalize, sort, and merge overlapping/duplicate (start, end) ranges.
83	
84	        A highlight reel must never replay the same footage. The same rally can land in
85	        the segment list more than once — e.g. two detector runs accumulated for one
86	        video (add_video is an UPSERT, so re-running without a prune leaves both sets), or
87	        chunk-overlap duplicates near boundaries. Stitching those verbatim plays the rally
88	        twice. Merging any range that overlaps or abuts the previous one collapses true
89	        duplicates/overlaps while preserving genuinely distinct (gapped) rallies.
90	        Degenerate rows (end <= start) are dropped. Timestamps may be float/int/str.
91	        """
92	        parsed = []
93	        for raw_s, raw_e in segments:
94	            s = VideoCompiler._parse_time(raw_s)
95	            e = VideoCompiler._parse_time(raw_e)
96	            if e > s:
97	                parsed.append((s, e))
98	        parsed.sort()
99	        merged: List[Tuple[float, float]] = []
100	        for s, e in parsed:
101	            if merged and s <= merged[-1][1]:   # overlaps/abuts previous -> extend it
102	                merged[-1] = (merged[-1][0], max(merged[-1][1], e))
103	            else:
104	                merged.append((s, e))
105	        return merged
106	
107	    def _get_video_duration(self, video_path: str) -> float:
108	        """Probes the video file to get its total duration in seconds.
109	        Returns 0.0 if it cannot be determined."""
110	        try:
111	            result = subprocess.run(
112	                [
113	                    "ffprobe", "-v", "error",
114	                    "-show_entries", "format=duration",
115	                    "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1",
116	                    video_path
117	                ],
118	                capture_output=True, text=True, check=True
119	            )
120	            return float(result.stdout.strip())
121	        except Exception as e:
122	            logger.warning(f"Could not determine video duration via ffprobe: {e}")
123	            return 0.0

128	        watermarking is disabled or has nothing to draw. The concat stage stays -c copy
129	        because the mark is already baked into every clip identically."""
130	        wm = self.watermark
131	        if not wm or not wm.get("enabled"):
132	            return None
133	
134	        text = (wm.get("text") or "").strip()
135	        logo_path = (wm.get("logo_path") or "").strip()
136	        if logo_path and not os.path.exists(logo_path):
137	            logger.warning(f"[watermark] logo not found: {logo_path} — skipping logo overlay.")
138	            logo_path = ""
139	        if not text and not logo_path:
140	            return None
141	
142	        margin = int(wm.get("margin", 24))
143	        opacity = float(wm.get("opacity", 0.85))
144	        position = str(wm.get("position", "bottom-right"))
145	        # drawtext uses lowercase w/h (main) + tw/th (text); overlay uses W/H (main) + w/h (logo).
146	        dt_xy = {
147	            "bottom-right": f"x=w-tw-{margin}:y=h-th-{margin}",
148	            "bottom-left": f"x={margin}:y=h-th-{margin}",
149	            "top-right": f"x=w-tw-{margin}:y={margin}",
150	            "top-left": f"x={margin}:y={margin}",
151	        }.get(position, f"x=w-tw-{margin}:y=h-th-{margin}")
152	        ov_xy = {
153	            "bottom-right": f"W-w-{margin}:H-h-{margin}",
154	            "bottom-left": f"{margin}:H-h-{margin}",
155	            "top-right": f"W-w-{margin}:{margin}",
156	            "top-left": f"{margin}:{margin}",
157	        }.get(position, f"W-w-{margin}:H-h-{margin}")
158	
159	        drawtext = ""
160	        if text:
161	            ratio = float(wm.get("font_size_ratio") or 0.035)
162	            divisor = max(1, round(1.0 / ratio))
163	            # textfile= sidesteps the notorious drawtext text= escaping for arbitrary strings.
164	            tf_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "watermark.txt")
165	            with open(tf_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
166	                fh.write(text)
167	            # Font precedence: explicit font_path → bundled Apache-2.0 default (renders
168	            # without fontconfig) → fontconfig family (last-resort, environment-dependent).
169	            font_path = (wm.get("font_path") or "").strip()
170	            if not font_path and _BUNDLED_FONT.exists():
171	                font_path = str(_BUNDLED_FONT)
172	            if font_path:
173	                font_opt = f"fontfile='{self._ff_quote(font_path)}'"
174	            else:
175	                font_opt = f"font='{wm.get('font', 'Sans')}'"
176	            box = ""
177	            if wm.get("box", True):
178	                box = f":box=1:boxcolor=black@{min(opacity, 0.5):.2f}:boxborderw=10"
179	            drawtext = (
180	                f"drawtext=textfile='{self._ff_quote(tf_path)}':{font_opt}"
181	                f":fontcolor=white@{opacity:.2f}:fontsize=h/{divisor}:{dt_xy}{box}"
182	            )
183	
184	        if logo_path and drawtext:
185	            return f"movie='{self._ff_quote(logo_path)}'[wm];[in][wm]overlay={ov_xy},{drawtext}"
186	        if logo_path:
187	            return f"movie='{self._ff_quote(logo_path)}'[wm];[in][wm]overlay={ov_xy}"
188	        return drawtext
189	
190	    def _trim_one_segment(
191	        self,
192	        idx: int,
193	        raw_start: Union[int, float, str],
194	        raw_end: Union[int, float, str],
195	        segments: List[Tuple[float, float]],
196	        video_duration: float,
197	        tmp_dir: str,
198	        raw_video_path: str,
199	        watermark_filter: Optional[str],
200	    ) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[Tuple[int, float, float, str]]]:
201	        """Trim a single segment into its own clip file (extracted verbatim from the
202	        per-segment body of compile_highlights). Returns (clip_path, skip_record): on
203	        success the clip path and None; on a skip (invalid/out-of-range/ffmpeg failure)
204	        None and the (idx, start, end, reason) record to record in skipped_segments."""
205	        # Normalize timestamps to float seconds — handles float, int,
206	        # plain numeric strings, and colon-separated formats like "MM:SS"
207	        start = self._parse_time(raw_start)
208	        end = self._parse_time(raw_end)
209	        segment_label = f"Segment #{idx+1}/{len(segments)} [{start:.2f}s - {end:.2f}s]"
210	
211	        # Validate the segment times
212	        if start < 0:
213	            logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: start_time is negative ({start:.2f}s). Clamping to 0.")
214	            start = 0.0
215	
216	        if start >= end:
217	            logger.error(f"{segment_label}: start_time ({start:.2f}s) >= end_time ({end:.2f}s). Skipping invalid segment.")
218	            return None, (idx, start, end, "start >= end")
219	
220	        # Check if segment extends beyond video duration
221	        if video_duration > 0:
222	            if start >= video_duration:
223	                logger.error(f"{segment_label}: start_time ({start:.2f}s) is beyond the video duration ({video_duration:.2f}s). "
224	                             f"This segment cannot be extracted — the video ran out. Skipping.")
225	                return None, (idx, start, end, "start beyond video end")
226	
227	            if end > video_duration:
228	                original_end = end
229	                end = video_duration
230	                logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: end_time ({original_end:.2f}s) exceeds video duration ({video_duration:.2f}s). "
231	                               f"Clamping end_time to {video_duration:.2f}s. The segment may be truncated.")
232	
233	        clip_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, f"clip_{idx}.mp4")
234	
235	        # Precise sub-second trim using fast re-encoding to preserve stream headers
236	        padded_start = max(0.0, start - self.begin_padding)
237	        padded_end = end + self.end_padding
238	
239	        # Clamp padded_end to video duration if known
240	        if video_duration > 0 and padded_end > video_duration:
241	            padded_end = video_duration
242	            logger.info(f"{segment_label}: Padded end ({end + self.end_padding:.2f}s) exceeds video duration. "
243	                        f"Clamping to {video_duration:.2f}s (end padding partially applied).")
244	
245	        trim_duration = padded_end - padded_start
246	        logger.info(f"Trimming {segment_label} -> [{padded_start:.3f}s - {padded_end:.3f}s] (duration: {trim_duration:.2f}s)")
247	
248	        base_cmd = [
249	            "ffmpeg", "-y",
250	            "-ss", str(round(padded_start, 3)),
251	            "-to", str(round(padded_end, 3)),
252	            "-i", raw_video_path,
253	            "-c:v", "libx264",
254	            "-preset", "superfast",
255	            "-crf", "22",
256	            "-c:a", "aac",
257	            "-b:a", "128k",
258	        ]
259	        vf_args = ["-vf", watermark_filter] if watermark_filter else []
260	
261	        # Try with the watermark first; if that encode fails (e.g. a bad font/logo
262	        # path), retry once WITHOUT it so a branding misconfig can never nuke the
263	        # whole reel. Clips stay codec-identical either way, so concat -c copy holds.
264	        attempts = [vf_args, []] if vf_args else [[]]
265	        produced = False
266	        last_exc: Optional[subprocess.CalledProcessError] = None
267	        cmd = base_cmd + [clip_path]
268	        for attempt_vf in attempts:
269	            cmd = base_cmd + attempt_vf + [clip_path]
270	            try:
271	                subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
272	            except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
273	                last_exc = e
274	                if attempt_vf and vf_args:
275	                    _err = (e.stderr.decode(errors="replace").strip()[-300:]
276	                            if getattr(e, "stderr", None) else "")
277	                    logger.warning(
278	                        f"{segment_label}: watermark encode failed; retrying without it. "
279	                        f"ffmpeg: {_err or '(no stderr captured)'}"
280	                    )
281	                    continue
282	                break
283	            if os.path.exists(clip_path) and os.path.getsize(clip_path) > 0:
284	                produced = True
285	                if not attempt_vf and vf_args:
286	                    logger.warning(f"{segment_label}: encoded WITHOUT the watermark (the watermark filter failed).")
287	                break
288	            last_exc = None  # ffmpeg succeeded but produced nothing
289	            break
290	
291	        if produced:
292	            return clip_path, None
293	        elif last_exc is not None:
294	            stderr_msg = last_exc.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()
295	            logger.error(f"{segment_label}: FFmpeg trim failed.")
296	            print(f"  Command: {' '.join(cmd)}")
297	            print(f"  FFmpeg stderr: {stderr_msg[-500:]}")  # Last 500 chars to avoid flooding
298	            return None, (idx, start, end, f"ffmpeg error: {stderr_msg[-200:]}")
299	        else:
300	            logger.error(f"{segment_label}: FFmpeg exited successfully but output clip is missing or empty. Skipping.")
301	            return None, (idx, start, end, "empty output clip")
302	
303	    def compile_highlights(self, raw_video_path: str, segments: List[Tuple[float, float]], output_filename: str) -> str:
304	        """
305	        Extracts selected segments from a raw video and stitches them together
306	        into a seamless highlights file, applying end_padding to prevent abrupt endings.
307	        """
308	        if not segments:
309	            raise ValueError("No highlight segments provided to compile.")
310	
311	        # Never stitch the same footage twice: collapse overlapping/duplicate ranges
312	        # (e.g. accumulated re-run segments or chunk-overlap dupes) before trimming.
313	        original_count = len(segments)
314	        segments = self._merge_overlapping(segments)
315	        if len(segments) < original_count:
316	            logger.info("Merged %d overlapping/duplicate segment(s) before stitching: %d -> %d.",
317	                        original_count - len(segments), original_count, len(segments))
318	        if not segments:
319	            raise ValueError("No valid (start < end) highlight segments after merge.")
320	
321	        if not os.path.exists(raw_video_path):
322	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"[COMPILER ERROR] Source video file not found: {raw_video_path}")
323	
324	        # Defense-in-depth: never let the output name escape output_dir (the API also
325	        # validates this at the request boundary).
326	        output_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, os.path.basename(output_filename))
327	
328	        # Probe video duration so we can detect out-of-range segments
329	        video_duration = self._get_video_duration(raw_video_path)
330	        if video_duration > 0:
331	            logger.info(f"Source video duration: {video_duration:.2f}s")
332	        else:
333	            logger.warning("Video duration unknown. Cannot validate segment boundaries against video length.")
334	
335	        # We will create temporary clips and stitch them using FFmpeg concat demuxer
336	        temp_clips = []
337	        skipped_segments = []
338	
339	        # Create a temp directory within output_dir
340	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=self.output_dir) as tmp_dir:
341	            logger.info(f"Trimming {len(segments)} segments (begin_padding={self.begin_padding}s, end_padding={self.end_padding}s)...")
342	            # Build the (optional) watermark filtergraph once; it is applied during every
343	            # per-clip re-encode so the mark is baked into each clip identically.
344	            watermark_filter = self._watermark_filter(tmp_dir)
345	            if watermark_filter:
346	                logger.info("[watermark] applying branding overlay to each clip.")
347	            for idx, (raw_start, raw_end) in enumerate(segments):
348	                clip_path, skip_record = self._trim_one_segment(
349	                    idx, raw_start, raw_end, segments, video_duration,
350	                    tmp_dir, raw_video_path, watermark_filter,
351	                )
352	                if clip_path is not None:
353	                    temp_clips.append(clip_path)
354	                if skip_record is not None:
355	                    skipped_segments.append(skip_record)
356	
357	            # Report summary of skipped segments
358	            if skipped_segments:
359	                logger.info(f"\n[SUMMARY] {len(skipped_segments)} out of {len(segments)} segments were SKIPPED:")
360	                for seg_idx, seg_start, seg_end, reason in skipped_segments:
361	                    logger.info(f"  - Segment #{seg_idx+1} [{seg_start:.2f}s - {seg_end:.2f}s]: {reason}")
362	                logger.info("")
363	
364	            if not temp_clips:
365	                raise RuntimeError(
366	                    f"[COMPILER ERROR] Failed to generate ANY valid highlight clips. "
367	                    f"All {len(segments)} segments were skipped. "
368	                    f"Check the errors above for details. "
369	                    f"Common causes: video file is corrupted, segments are out of range, or ffmpeg is not installed."
370	                )
371	
372	            logger.info(f"Successfully trimmed {len(temp_clips)}/{len(segments)} clips. Preparing to stitch...")
373	
374	            # Create a list file for FFmpeg concat demuxer
375	            list_file_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, "clips.txt")
376	            # encoding pinned: a non-ASCII clip path would crash on Windows (cp1252 default);
377	            # ffmpeg's concat demuxer reads the list as UTF-8 on every OS.
378	            with open(list_file_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
379	                for clip in temp_clips:
380	                    # Escape single quotes in path
381	                    safe_path = clip.replace("'", "'\\''")
382	                    f.write(f"file '{safe_path}'\n")
383	
384	            logger.info("Stitching clips together...")
385	            # Stitch them together without re-encoding (since they were encoded with identical settings)
386	            concat_cmd = [
387	                "ffmpeg", "-y",
388	                "-f", "concat",
389	                "-safe", "0",
390	                "-i", list_file_path,
391	                "-c", "copy",
392	                output_path
393	            ]
394	
395	            try:
396	                subprocess.run(concat_cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
397	
398	                # Final verification
399	                if not os.path.exists(output_path) or os.path.getsize(output_path) == 0:
400	                    raise RuntimeError(
401	                        f"[COMPILER ERROR] FFmpeg concatenation appeared to succeed, "
402	                        f"but output file is missing or empty: {output_path}"
403	                    )
404	
405	                output_size_mb = os.path.getsize(output_path) / (1024 * 1024)
406	                logger.info(f"Dynamic highlight reel saved: {output_path} ({output_size_mb:.1f} MB)")
407	                return output_path
408	            except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
409	                stderr_msg = e.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()
410	                raise RuntimeError(
411	                    f"[COMPILER ERROR] FFmpeg concatenation failed.\n"
412	                    f"  Command: {' '.join(concat_cmd)}\n"
413	                    f"  FFmpeg stderr: {stderr_msg[-500:]}"
414	                ) from e

### 547. assistant (2026-06-21T18:04:39.087Z)

I now have the complete engine picture. Key facts driving the design:
- **Watermark** = ffmpeg `drawtext` with `text` + `font_path` (defaults to bundled **RobotoSlab, Latin-only**), built at `/api/compile` from `global_config.stitching.watermark`.
- **`CompileRequest`** (video_id, segment_ids, output_filename) and **`ProcessRequest`** (video_path, …) — neither carries locale.
- **DB**: migration head **v6** on origin/master; v7 is free but the serving session plans its own v7 → real collision risk.
- **Fonts**: only RobotoSlab is bundled → es/da/id work as-is, but **kn/hi need Noto Sans Kannada/Devanagari `.ttf`** bundled.

The font availability decides the kn/hi approach. Let me check whether I can source the Noto `.ttf` (from @fontsource in node_modules, or a download):

Here's the spec, then I'll build it.

## Spec — locale capture + localized watermarks

**Architecture (deliberately decoupled, to ship the watermark without waiting on a DB migration):**

| Concern | How | Repo | Migration? |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Localized watermark** | `CompileRequest.locale` → a `localize_watermark(cfg, locale)` helper overrides `text` + `font_path` per locale → ffmpeg `drawtext`. en/es/da/id use RobotoSlab; kn/hi use bundled Noto. | engine | **No** |
| **SPA sends locale** | pass `i18n.resolvedLanguage` to `/api/compile` (watermark) **and** `/api/process` (analytics) | khelsutra | — |
| **PMF analytics** | `ProcessRequest.locale` → `jobs.locale` column → `SELECT locale, COUNT(*) FROM jobs GROUP BY locale` | engine | **v7 (the collision-risk piece)** |

**3 PRs, lowest-risk first:** PR-1 watermark (engine, no migration) → PR-2 SPA (no engine collision) → PR-3 analytics v7 migration (engine, **done last + in an isolated worktree + re-verified against `origin/master`** so it can't collide with the serving session's planned v7 — renumber to v8 if theirs lands first). Sending `locale` to `/api/process` before PR-3 is harmless (Pydantic ignores unknown fields).

**Watermark fallback (honest):** unknown/missing locale → the existing default text+font (en behavior unchanged). If a Noto font is somehow absent, the compiler already retries without the watermark rather than failing the reel.

Fonts shipped with the highlight **watermark** (`stitching.watermark`). All bundled fonts
are **Apache-2.0** — commercial-clean per the project licensing guardrail (MIT/Apache/BSD
only), so they can be redistributed with the tool and self-hosted derivatives.

`RobotoSlab-Regular.ttf` is the **default** watermark font: `stitching.watermark.font_path`
defaults to it so the overlay renders **without a system fontconfig setup** (a common gap
on Windows, where `font='Sans'` fails). To use a different typeface — your own, or another
bundled one — set `stitching.watermark.font_path` to its path in `config.json`.

### 567. assistant (2026-06-21T18:09:27.616Z)

"""Localized reel-watermark text + font selection.

The SPA passes the UI locale to ``POST /api/compile``; this maps it to a localized branding string
and a script-appropriate bundled font, so a Kannada/Hindi/… user's reel carries the watermark in
their language. The brand token ("KhelSutra.guru") stays Latin; only the surrounding phrase localizes.

Fonts (``backend/assets/fonts/``):
  - en/es/da/id (Latin)  → the configured default (bundled RobotoSlab, Apache-2.0)
  - kn → Noto Sans Kannada · hi → Noto Sans Devanagari  (OFL-1.1, bundled — the D7 OFL-for-media
    carve-out; FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL §4 anticipated this).

Honest fallbacks: an unknown/missing locale (or a locale with no entry, incl. ``en``) returns the
watermark UNCHANGED, so existing/default behavior is preserved. If a script font file is somehow
absent, ``font_path`` is left at the default (RobotoSlab) rather than pointing at a missing file —
and the compiler already retries the encode without the watermark if drawtext can't render, so a
font gap can never nuke the reel. Kept config-package-agnostic (operates on a dict).
"""
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

# This module is backend/pipeline/stitcher/ → parents[2] == backend/ (matches compiler.py).
_FONTS_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "assets" / "fonts"

# Localized watermark phrase per locale. AI-drafted → native-speaker review pending (same posture as
# the SPA catalogs). `en` is intentionally absent: it falls through to the configured default text.
WATERMARK_TEXT: Dict[str, str] = {
    "es": "Generado con KhelSutra.guru",
    "da": "Lavet med KhelSutra.guru",
    "id": "Dibuat dengan KhelSutra.guru",
    "kn": "KhelSutra.guru ನಿಂದ ರಚಿಸಲಾಗಿದೆ",
    "hi": "KhelSutra.guru से बनाया गया",
}

# Script-specific bundled fonts — only where the Latin default (RobotoSlab) can't render the script.
# Latin locales (es/da/id) localize the TEXT but keep the default font.
FONT_FILE_BY_LOCALE: Dict[str, str] = {
    "kn": "NotoSansKannada-Variable.ttf",
    "hi": "NotoSansDevanagari-Variable.ttf",
}


def _base_locale(locale: Optional[str]) -> str:
    """Normalize a BCP-47 tag to its base subtag: 'kn-IN'/'kn_IN'/'KN' → 'kn'."""
    return (locale or "").strip().lower().replace("_", "-").split("-")[0]


def localize_watermark(watermark: Any, locale: Optional[str]) -> Any:
    """Return a watermark dict with ``text`` (and ``font_path`` for non-Latin scripts) localized for
    ``locale``. Returns the input UNCHANGED for ``en`` / unknown / missing locale (default behavior).

    Accepts a ``WatermarkConfig`` model or a plain dict; returns a dict when it localizes (the shape
    ``VideoCompiler`` already accepts), or the original object untouched when there's nothing to do.
    """
    base = _base_locale(locale)
    if base not in WATERMARK_TEXT:
        return watermark  # en / unsupported / missing → configured default, unchanged

    if hasattr(watermark, "model_dump"):
        wm: Dict[str, Any] = watermark.model_dump()
    elif isinstance(watermark, dict):
        wm = dict(watermark)
    else:
        return watermark  # unknown shape — don't risk mangling it

    wm["text"] = WATERMARK_TEXT[base]
    font_file = FONT_FILE_BY_LOCALE.get(base)
    if font_file:
        font_path = _FONTS_DIR / font_file
        if font_path.exists():
            wm["font_path"] = str(font_path)
        # else: leave the configured/default font_path (RobotoSlab) — a graceful Latin fallback.
    return wm

### 581. assistant (2026-06-21T18:11:04.681Z)

# Bundled fonts

Fonts shipped with the highlight **watermark** (`stitching.watermark`), used by ffmpeg `drawtext`
during the per-clip re-encode (`backend/pipeline/stitcher/compiler.py`).

| File | Font | License | Used for | Source |
|------|------|---------|----------|--------|
| `RobotoSlab-Regular.ttf` | Roboto Slab | Apache-2.0 (`LICENSE-Apache-2.0.txt`) | **default** watermark; Latin locales (en/es/da/id) | https://github.com/google/fonts/tree/main/apache/robotoslab |
| `NotoSansKannada-Variable.ttf` | Noto Sans Kannada | OFL-1.1 (`LICENSE-OFL-1.1.txt`) | Kannada (`kn`) watermark | https://github.com/google/fonts/tree/main/ofl/notosanskannada |
| `NotoSansDevanagari-Variable.ttf` | Noto Sans Devanagari | OFL-1.1 (`LICENSE-OFL-1.1.txt`) | Hindi (`hi`) watermark | https://github.com/google/fonts/tree/main/ofl/notosansdevanagari |

`RobotoSlab-Regular.ttf` is the **default** watermark font: `stitching.watermark.font_path`
defaults to it so the overlay renders **without a system fontconfig setup** (a common gap on
Windows, where `font='Sans'` fails). The **localized** watermark (`backend/pipeline/stitcher/
watermark_i18n.py`, driven by the UI locale on `POST /api/compile`) keeps RobotoSlab for Latin
locales and switches to the script-appropriate Noto font for `kn`/`hi`. The Noto fonts also include
basic Latin, so the Latin brand token ("KhelSutra.guru") renders correctly alongside the local script.

## Licensing

Code/data/weights stay **MIT/Apache/BSD** per the project guardrail. **Fonts are media, not code** —
the **OFL-for-media carve-out (FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL D7, owner-ratified 2026-06-21)** permits
bundling/redistributing **SIL OFL-1.1** fonts (the same Noto faces the SPA self-hosts). To add another
bundled font: drop the `.ttf` here, keep it **MIT/Apache/BSD *or* OFL-1.1**, ship its license file, and
add a row above.

*Roboto Slab is © Google, licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0. Noto Sans Kannada and Noto
Sans Devanagari are © The Noto Project Authors, licensed under the SIL Open Font License, Version 1.1.*

22	def test_watermark_config_defaults_on_with_brand_text():
23	    wm = AppConfig().stitching.watermark
24	    assert wm.enabled is True  # default-on, with an off switch
25	    assert wm.text == "Generated by Khelsutra.guru"
26	    assert wm.position == "bottom-right"

30	    cfg = AppConfig.model_validate({"stitching": {"watermark": {"enabled": False}}})
31	    assert cfg.stitching.watermark.enabled is False
32	
33	
34	def test_font_size_ratio_and_opacity_bounds_enforced():
35	    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
36	        WatermarkConfig(opacity=1.5)
37	    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
38	        WatermarkConfig(font_size_ratio=0.0)
39	
40	
41	# --- filtergraph builder ------------------------------------------------------
42	
43	def test_disabled_or_none_builds_no_filter(tmp_path):
44	    assert VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "a"), watermark=None)._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path)) is None
45	    off = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "b"), watermark={"enabled": False})
46	    assert off._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path)) is None
47	
48	
49	def test_text_filter_writes_configurable_string_and_builds_drawtext(tmp_path):
50	    custom = "My Academy 2026 • Khelsutra.guru"
51	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o"), watermark={"enabled": True, "text": custom})
52	    graph = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
53	    assert graph is not None and graph.startswith("drawtext=")
54	    # The arbitrary, configurable string is written verbatim to the textfile (no escaping pain).
55	    assert (tmp_path / "watermark.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == custom
56	    assert "textfile=" in graph
57	    assert "fontcolor=white@0.85" in graph
58	    assert "fontsize=h/29" in graph                 # round(1/0.035) == 29
59	    assert "x=w-tw-24:y=h-th-24" in graph           # bottom-right default, margin 24
60	    assert "box=1" in graph
61	
62	
63	def test_position_and_margin_map_to_corner(tmp_path):
64	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o"),
65	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X", "position": "top-left", "margin": 12})
66	    graph = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
67	    assert "x=12:y=12" in graph

71	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o1"),
72	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X", "font_path": "/fonts/Brand.ttf"})
73	    g = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
74	    assert "fontfile='/fonts/Brand.ttf'" in g

78	    # No font_path → the bundled Apache-2.0 Roboto Slab (renders without fontconfig).
79	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o2"), watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X"})

81	    assert "fontfile=" in g and "RobotoSlab-Regular.ttf" in g
82	    assert "font='" not in g  # not the fontconfig path

85	def test_bundled_font_actually_ships_and_is_apache():
86	    import backend.pipeline.stitcher.compiler as cmod
87	    assert cmod._BUNDLED_FONT.exists(), "bundled default watermark font must ship in the repo"

90	    lic = cmod._BUNDLED_FONT.parent / "LICENSE-Apache-2.0.txt"
91	    assert lic.exists() and "Apache License" in lic.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

94	def test_fontconfig_fallback_when_bundled_missing(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
95	    import backend.pipeline.stitcher.compiler as cmod
96	    monkeypatch.setattr(cmod, "_BUNDLED_FONT", Path("/no/such/font.ttf"))
97	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o3"),
98	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X", "font": "DejaVu Sans"})
99	    g = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
100	    assert "font='DejaVu Sans'" in g and "fontfile=" not in g

102	
103	# --- Windows drive-letter colon escaping (regression) -------------------------
104	# A Windows absolute path's drive-letter colon (C:/...) must be escaped for the
105	# ffmpeg filtergraph, or drawtext/movie parse ':' as an option separator and the
106	# encode fails with "Invalid argument" — which silently disabled the on-by-default
107	# bundled-font watermark (and absolute logo paths) on Windows. Verified end-to-end
108	# against real ffmpeg before this fix landed.
109	
110	def test_ff_quote_escapes_drive_letter_colon():
111	    assert VideoCompiler._ff_quote(r"C:\fonts\Brand.ttf") == r"C\:/fonts/Brand.ttf"
112	    # no colon -> unchanged (POSIX paths and the Linux bundled font are unaffected)
113	    assert VideoCompiler._ff_quote("/usr/share/fonts/font.ttf") == "/usr/share/fonts/font.ttf"
114	    # colon + single-quote + backslash all handled
115	    assert VideoCompiler._ff_quote(r"D:\a'b\f.ttf") == r"D\:/a\'b/f.ttf"
116	
117	
118	def test_watermark_font_path_with_drive_colon_is_escaped(tmp_path):
119	    # Explicit font_path (literal, existence not required) -> deterministic on any OS.
120	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o"),
121	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X", "font_path": "C:/fonts/Brand.ttf"})
122	    g = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
123	    assert r"fontfile='C\:/fonts/Brand.ttf'" in g
124	    assert "fontfile='C:/fonts" not in g  # the raw colon would break drawtext parsing
125	
126	
127	def test_logo_plus_text_overlays_then_draws(tmp_path):
128	    logo = tmp_path / "logo.png"
129	    logo.write_bytes(b"\x89PNG\r\n")
130	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o"),
131	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X", "logo_path": str(logo)})
132	    graph = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
133	    assert graph.startswith("movie=")
134	    assert "overlay=W-w-24:H-h-24" in graph
135	    assert "drawtext=" in graph
136	
137	
138	def test_missing_logo_is_skipped_gracefully(tmp_path):
139	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o"),
140	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "X", "logo_path": str(tmp_path / "nope.png")})
141	    graph = comp._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
142	    assert "movie=" not in graph and graph.startswith("drawtext=")  # text still drawn
143

145	# --- end-to-end wiring (ffmpeg/ffprobe mocked) --------------------------------
146	
147	def _fake_run_factory(calls, fail_on_vf=False):
148	    def fake_run(cmd, *a, **k):
149	        calls.append(cmd)
150	        if cmd[0] == "ffprobe":
151	            return MagicMock(stdout="100.0\n", returncode=0)
152	        if fail_on_vf and "-vf" in cmd:
153	            raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(1, cmd, output=b"", stderr=b"font not found")
154	        out = cmd[-1]
155	        if isinstance(out, str) and out.endswith(".mp4"):
156	            open(out, "wb").write(b"clip")
157	        return MagicMock(returncode=0, stdout=b"", stderr=b"")
158	    return fake_run
159	
160	
161	def test_compile_applies_watermark_vf_to_each_clip_not_concat(tmp_path):
162	    raw = tmp_path / "raw.mp4"
163	    raw.write_bytes(b"x")
164	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "out"),
165	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "Generated by Khelsutra.guru"})
166	    calls = []
167	    with patch.object(subprocess, "run", side_effect=_fake_run_factory(calls)):
168	        comp.compile_highlights(str(raw), [(0.0, 5.0), (10.0, 15.0)], "reel.mp4")
169	
170	    trim_cmds = [c for c in calls if c[0] == "ffmpeg" and "-i" in c and "concat" not in c]
171	    assert trim_cmds, "expected per-clip trim invocations"
172	    for c in trim_cmds:
173	        assert "-vf" in c
174	        assert "drawtext=" in c[c.index("-vf") + 1]
175	    # The concat (-c copy) must NOT re-filter — the mark is already baked into each clip.
176	    concat_cmds = [c for c in calls if "concat" in c]
177	    assert concat_cmds and all("-vf" not in c for c in concat_cmds)
178	
179	
180	def test_compile_without_watermark_adds_no_vf(tmp_path):
181	    raw = tmp_path / "raw.mp4"
182	    raw.write_bytes(b"x")
183	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "out"))  # no watermark passed
184	    calls = []
185	    with patch.object(subprocess, "run", side_effect=_fake_run_factory(calls)):
186	        comp.compile_highlights(str(raw), [(0.0, 5.0)], "reel.mp4")
187	    assert all("-vf" not in c for c in calls)
188	
189	
190	def test_watermark_failure_falls_back_to_unwatermarked_clip(tmp_path):
191	    """A bad font/logo must not nuke the reel: the clip re-encodes without the watermark."""
192	    raw = tmp_path / "raw.mp4"
193	    raw.write_bytes(b"x")
194	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "out"),
195	                         watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "Generated by Khelsutra.guru"})
196	    calls = []
197	    with patch.object(subprocess, "run", side_effect=_fake_run_factory(calls, fail_on_vf=True)):
198	        out_path = comp.compile_highlights(str(raw), [(0.0, 5.0)], "reel.mp4")
199	
200	    # Reel still produced despite the watermark encode failing.
201	    assert out_path.endswith("reel.mp4")
202	    assert os.path.exists(out_path)
203	    # For the clip, both a watermarked attempt and a plain fallback attempt were made.
204	    clip_cmds = [c for c in calls if c[0] == "ffmpeg" and "concat" not in c and "-i" in c]
205	    assert any("-vf" in c for c in clip_cmds)       # watermark attempt
206	    assert any("-vf" not in c for c in clip_cmds)   # fallback attempt
207	
208	
209	def test_watermark_failure_logs_the_ffmpeg_reason(tmp_path, caplog):
210	    """The fallback warning must surface ffmpeg's stderr (the *why*), not go silent —
211	    e.g. the 'Fontconfig error' that tells the user to set font_path."""
212	    import logging as _logging
213	    raw = tmp_path / "raw.mp4"
214	    raw.write_bytes(b"x")
215	    comp = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "out"), watermark={"enabled": True, "text": "Y"})
216	
217	    def fake_run(cmd, *a, **k):
218	        if cmd[0] == "ffprobe":
219	            return MagicMock(stdout="100.0\n", returncode=0)
220	        if "-vf" in cmd:
221	            raise subprocess.CalledProcessError(
222	                1, cmd, output=b"", stderr=b"Fontconfig error: Cannot load default config file")
223	        out = cmd[-1]
224	        if isinstance(out, str) and out.endswith(".mp4"):
225	            open(out, "wb").write(b"clip")
226	        return MagicMock(returncode=0, stdout=b"", stderr=b"")
227	
228	    with caplog.at_level(_logging.WARNING):
229	        with patch.object(subprocess, "run", side_effect=fake_run):
230	            comp.compile_highlights(str(raw), [(0.0, 5.0)], "reel.mp4")
231	    assert "Fontconfig error" in caplog.text

### 590. assistant (2026-06-21T18:12:39.856Z)

"""Tests for the localized reel watermark (backend/pipeline/stitcher/watermark_i18n.py).

The SPA passes the UI locale to /api/compile; localize_watermark maps it to a localized branding
string + a script-appropriate bundled font. Covers: en/unknown/None pass-through (default unchanged),
Latin locales (text-only override), kn/hi (text + the bundled Noto font), BCP-47 base-subtag
normalization, model-and-dict inputs without mutation, the bundled Noto fonts actually shipping
(OFL), and that a localized watermark flows correctly into the ffmpeg drawtext filtergraph.
"""
from pathlib import Path

from backend.config.models import WatermarkConfig
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.compiler import VideoCompiler
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.watermark_i18n import (
    FONT_FILE_BY_LOCALE,
    WATERMARK_TEXT,
    _FONTS_DIR,
    localize_watermark,
)


# --- pass-through (default behavior preserved) --------------------------------

def test_en_unknown_none_pass_through_unchanged():
    cfg = WatermarkConfig()
    # en / unsupported / missing → the SAME object back (configured default text + font untouched).
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "en") is cfg
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "en-US") is cfg
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "fr") is cfg  # supported by neither the watermark nor the SPA
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, None) is cfg
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "") is cfg


# --- Latin locales: text localized, font stays the default --------------------

def test_latin_locales_override_text_only():
    cfg = WatermarkConfig()
    for loc in ("es", "da", "id"):
        wm = localize_watermark(cfg, loc)
        assert isinstance(wm, dict)
        assert wm["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT[loc]
        # No script font override → keeps the configured default (empty → RobotoSlab in the compiler).
        assert wm.get("font_path", "") == cfg.font_path
        assert "Noto" not in wm.get("font_path", "")


# --- kn/hi: text localized + the bundled Noto font ----------------------------

def test_indic_locales_set_text_and_bundled_noto_font():
    cfg = WatermarkConfig()
    for loc, font_file in FONT_FILE_BY_LOCALE.items():  # kn, hi
        wm = localize_watermark(cfg, loc)
        assert wm["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT[loc]
        assert wm["font_path"].endswith(font_file)
        assert Path(wm["font_path"]).exists(), f"{loc} watermark font must ship"


def test_base_subtag_normalization():
    cfg = WatermarkConfig()
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "kn-IN")["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT["kn"]
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "hi_IN")["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT["hi"]
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "ES")["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT["es"]
    assert localize_watermark(cfg, "es-419")["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT["es"]  # Latin-American Spanish


# --- input handling: model + dict, no mutation --------------------------------

def test_accepts_dict_and_does_not_mutate_input():
    original = {"enabled": True, "text": "Generated by Khelsutra.guru", "font_path": ""}
    wm = localize_watermark(original, "kn")
    assert wm is not original
    assert original["text"] == "Generated by Khelsutra.guru"  # input untouched
    assert wm["text"] == WATERMARK_TEXT["kn"]


# --- bundled assets ship + are valid sfnt + OFL-licensed ----------------------

def test_bundled_noto_fonts_ship_and_are_valid_sfnt():
    for font_file in FONT_FILE_BY_LOCALE.values():
        fp = _FONTS_DIR / font_file
        assert fp.exists(), f"{font_file} must ship in backend/assets/fonts/"
        with open(fp, "rb") as fh:
            assert fh.read(4) in (b"\x00\x01\x00\x00", b"OTTO", b"true", b"ttcf")  # valid sfnt
    ofl = _FONTS_DIR / "LICENSE-OFL-1.1.txt"
    assert ofl.exists() and "Open Font License" in ofl.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")


# --- end-to-end: a localized watermark flows into the drawtext filtergraph -----

def test_localized_watermark_flows_into_drawtext_filtergraph(tmp_path):
    wm = localize_watermark(WatermarkConfig(enabled=True), "kn")
    graph = VideoCompiler(str(tmp_path / "o"), watermark=wm)._watermark_filter(str(tmp_path))
    assert graph is not None and "NotoSansKannada-Variable.ttf" in graph
    # The Kannada string is written verbatim to the drawtext textfile.
    assert (tmp_path / "watermark.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == WATERMARK_TEXT["kn"]

### 634. assistant (2026-06-21T18:22:03.100Z)

// LW2 — the SPA records the UI locale on the two engine calls that need it: /api/compile (localizes
// the reel watermark) and /api/process (PMF analytics). Optional + omitted when absent, so existing
// callers/back-compat are untouched.
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { compileReel, processVideo } from "./client";

type FetchFn = typeof fetch;
const ok = (json: unknown): FetchFn =>
  vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => json, text: async () => JSON.stringify(json) })) as unknown as FetchFn;
const bodyOf = (f: FetchFn) => {
  const calls = (f as unknown as { mock: { calls: [string, RequestInit][] } }).mock.calls;
  return JSON.parse(calls[calls.length - 1][1].body as string);
};

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
});

describe("LW2 locale passthrough", () => {
  it("compileReel includes the locale when given", async () => {
    const f = ok({ status: "success", filepath: "/o/r.mp4" });
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    await compileReel("vid", [1, 2], "r.mp4", "cid", "kn");
    expect(bodyOf(f)).toEqual({ video_id: "vid", segment_ids: [1, 2], output_filename: "r.mp4", locale: "kn" });
  });

  it("compileReel omits locale when absent (back-compat — no null/undefined in the body)", async () => {
    const f = ok({ status: "success", filepath: "/o/r.mp4" });
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    await compileReel("vid", [1], "r.mp4");
    expect(bodyOf(f)).toEqual({ video_id: "vid", segment_ids: [1], output_filename: "r.mp4" });
    expect("locale" in bodyOf(f)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("processVideo includes the locale when given, omits it when absent", async () => {
    const f = ok({ job_id: "j", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j" });
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    await processVideo("gs://b/x.mp4", "cid", "hi");
    expect(bodyOf(f)).toEqual({ video_path: "gs://b/x.mp4", locale: "hi" });

    await processVideo("gs://b/y.mp4", "cid");
    expect(bodyOf(f)).toEqual({ video_path: "gs://b/y.mp4" });
  });
});

### 665. user (2026-06-21T18:27:43.976Z)

64	    def _add_column_idempotent(cursor: sqlite3.Cursor, ddl: str) -> None:
65	        """Apply a bare ``ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN`` migration step re-runnably.
66	
67	        A DDL ``ALTER`` auto-commits independently of the surrounding ``with conn:``
68	        transaction, so an interrupted v3/v4 block can leave the column added while
69	        ``user_version`` stays un-bumped — and the next open would re-run the same
70	        ``ALTER`` and raise ``OperationalError: duplicate column name``, bricking every
71	        future ``Database(...)`` construction (``_init_db`` runs in ``__init__``). Swallow
72	        ONLY that specific error so the ladder stays crash-safe, matching the re-runnable
73	        ``CREATE ... IF NOT EXISTS`` v1/v2/v5 blocks.
74	        """
75	        try:
76	            cursor.execute(ddl)
77	        except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
78	            if "duplicate column name" not in str(exc).lower():
79	                raise
80

82	        """Initializes tables for videos, play_segments, and events."""
83	        with self._connect() as conn:

### 666. user (2026-06-21T18:27:44.788Z)

292	    def _migrate_to_v6(self, cursor):
293	        # v6: per-job COST LEDGER (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M8, D8). NULLABLE cost columns on
294	        # `jobs` (NULL = no cost recorded = byte-identical to today; nothing records until
295	        # billing.enabled + a real pricebook) + a VERSIONED `pricebook` table. Cost is stored in
296	        # USD (matches GCP billing = one source of truth); INR is a display conversion at a
297	        # configurable FX rate. ALTER (not recreate) so v1-v5 job rows survive; ADD COLUMNs are
298	        # idempotent so an interrupted upgrade can re-run them. Additive ONLY — no served change.
299	        for col, typ in (
300	            ("owner_id", "TEXT"),               # who pays (the non-owner user / tenant)
301	            ("compute_backend", "TEXT"),        # local_gpu | cloud_run | ...
302	            ("gpu_type", "TEXT"),               # L4 | T4 | ... (selects the per-second GPU rate)
303	            ("gpu_active_seconds", "REAL"),
304	            ("wall_seconds", "REAL"),
305	            ("bytes_out", "INTEGER"),           # egress
306	            ("gemini_cost_usd", "REAL"),        # provider-reported, already USD
307	            ("pricebook_version", "TEXT"),
308	            ("cost_usd", "REAL"),               # computed total (USD = source of truth)
309	            ("cost_breakdown_json", "TEXT"),    # {gpu_seconds: .., egress_gb: .., gemini_usd: ..}
310	        ):
311	            self._add_column_idempotent(cursor, f"ALTER TABLE jobs ADD COLUMN {col} {typ}")
312	        # Partial index keeps the NULL=no-cost fast path churn-free while making per-owner billing cheap.
313	        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_jobs_owner ON jobs(owner_id) "
314	                       "WHERE owner_id IS NOT NULL")
315	        # Versioned pricebook: one row per (version, resource, gpu_type). Rates change + differ by
316	        # gpu_type → re-version (never mutate a shipped version, so a recorded cost stays auditable).
317	        cursor.execute("""
318	            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pricebook (
319	                version TEXT NOT NULL,
320	                resource TEXT NOT NULL,
321	                gpu_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
322	                unit_price_usd REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
323	                currency TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USD',
324	                effective_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
325	            )
326	        """)
327	        cursor.execute("CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pricebook_key "
328	                       "ON pricebook(version, resource, gpu_type)")
329	        # Seed a PLACEHOLDER version with every rate 0.0 → cost_usd computes to 0 (nothing
330	        # user-facing changes). The owner INSERTs a real, calibrated version (real GCP $/GPU-s,
331	        # egress $/GB) and flips billing.pricebook_version to it — never overwrite this seed.
332	        for resource, gpu_type in (("gpu_seconds", "L4"), ("gpu_seconds", "T4"),
333	                                   ("wall_seconds", ""), ("egress_gb", "")):
334	            cursor.execute(
335	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO pricebook (version, resource, gpu_type, unit_price_usd, "
336	                "currency) VALUES ('placeholder-v0', ?, ?, 0.0, 'USD')", (resource, gpu_type))
337	        cursor.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 6")

def test_v6_migration_adds_cost_columns_and_seeds_placeholder(tmp_path):
    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "m.db"))
    with db._connect() as conn:
        ver = conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0]
        cols = {r[1] for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(jobs)").fetchall()}
        seeded = conn.execute(
            "SELECT unit_price_usd FROM pricebook WHERE version='placeholder-v0'").fetchall()
    assert ver >= 6
    assert {"cost_usd", "gpu_active_seconds", "wall_seconds", "bytes_out", "owner_id",
            "pricebook_version", "cost_breakdown_json", "compute_backend", "gpu_type"} <= cols
    assert len(seeded) >= 4 and all(r[0] == 0.0 for r in seeded)  # placeholder = every rate $0


def test_v6_migration_is_idempotent(tmp_path):
    """Re-opening the DB re-runs the ladder guard (version already 6 → no-op, no duplicate seed)."""
    path = str(tmp_path / "m.db")
    Database(path)
