### 705. assistant (2026-06-21T18:33:33.549Z)

"""v7 — job UI-locale capture + PMF analytics (which language markets use the tool).

Covers: the v7 migration (fresh DB + a v6→v7 in-place upgrade that back-fills locale=NULL on prior
rows), create_job storing the locale (NULL when absent), count_jobs_by_locale aggregation (incl. the
'(unset)' bucket for NULL), and the ProcessRequest.locale field. The /api/process end-to-end capture
lives in test_api_endpoints.py.
"""
import sqlite3

from backend.infrastructure.database import Database
from backend.main import ProcessRequest


def _cols(path, table):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        return {r[1] for r in conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})").fetchall()}
    finally:
        conn.close()


def _user_version(path):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        return conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0]
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_fresh_db_v7_has_jobs_locale_column(tmp_path):
    path = str(tmp_path / "fresh.db")
    Database(path)
    assert _user_version(path) == 7
    assert "locale" in _cols(path, "jobs")


def test_v6_db_upgrades_to_v7_and_backfills_locale_null(tmp_path):
    path = str(tmp_path / "v6.db")
    db = Database(path)  # born at v7
    db.create_job("j-old", "/v.mp4")  # a prior row, no locale
    # Simulate a real v6 jobs table (no locale column, version pinned at 6).
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    conn.execute("ALTER TABLE jobs DROP COLUMN locale")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version = 6")
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    assert "locale" not in _cols(path, "jobs")

    Database(path)  # re-open → the ladder runs _migrate_to_v7
    assert _user_version(path) == 7
    assert "locale" in _cols(path, "jobs")
    # The prior row survived the ALTER and back-filled to NULL (not dropped, not defaulted to a value).
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        assert conn.execute("SELECT locale FROM jobs WHERE id='j-old'").fetchone()[0] is None
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_create_job_stores_locale_and_defaults_null(tmp_path):
    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "j.db"))
    db.create_job("j-kn", "/a.mp4", locale="kn")
    db.create_job("j-none", "/b.mp4")  # no locale → NULL
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(tmp_path / "j.db"))
    try:
        assert conn.execute("SELECT locale FROM jobs WHERE id='j-kn'").fetchone()[0] == "kn"
        assert conn.execute("SELECT locale FROM jobs WHERE id='j-none'").fetchone()[0] is None
    finally:
        conn.close()


def test_count_jobs_by_locale_aggregates_with_unset_bucket(tmp_path):
    db = Database(str(tmp_path / "c.db"))
    for jid, loc in [("a", "kn"), ("b", "kn"), ("c", "hi"), ("d", None), ("e", "es")]:
        db.create_job(jid, "/v.mp4", locale=loc)
    counts = db.count_jobs_by_locale()
    assert counts == {"kn": 2, "hi": 1, "es": 1, "(unset)": 1}
    # Busiest-first ordering (a dict preserves insertion order in py3.7+).
    assert list(counts.keys())[0] == "kn"


def test_process_request_accepts_optional_locale():
    assert ProcessRequest(video_path="x", locale="hi-IN").locale == "hi-IN"
    assert ProcessRequest(video_path="x").locale is None

### 734. assistant (2026-06-21T18:43:07.230Z)

"""End-to-end watermark verification: generate a REAL highlight reel through the actual VideoCompiler
(per-clip re-encode + ffmpeg drawtext) for EVERY supported locale, then dump a cropped frame of the
bottom-right watermark for each so it can be eyeballed. Not committed (scratch)."""
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from backend.config.models import WatermarkConfig
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.compiler import VideoCompiler
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.watermark_i18n import WATERMARK_TEXT, localize_watermark

OUT = Path("scratch_wm_out")
OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
SRC = OUT / "source.mp4"

# A real 3s source with video + audio (the compile path re-encodes libx264 + aac).
subprocess.run([
    "ffmpeg", "-y",
    "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc=size=1280x720:rate=30:duration=3",
    "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "sine=frequency=440:duration=3",
    "-c:v", "libx264", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-c:a", "aac", "-shortest", str(SRC),
], check=True, capture_output=True)

locales = ["en", "kn", "hi", "es", "da", "id"]
results = []
for loc in locales:
    wm = localize_watermark(WatermarkConfig(enabled=True), loc)
    text = wm["text"] if isinstance(wm, dict) else wm.text
    comp = VideoCompiler(str(OUT), watermark=wm)
    reel = comp.compile_highlights(str(SRC), [(0.5, 2.5)], f"reel_{loc}.mp4")
    # Crop the bottom-right region where the watermark sits, so each locale is clearly legible.
    crop = OUT / f"wm_{loc}.png"
    subprocess.run([
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", reel, "-ss", "0.8",
        "-vf", "crop=820:110:in_w-840:in_h-130",
        "-frames:v", "1", str(crop),
    ], check=True, capture_output=True)
    size = Path(reel).stat().st_size
    results.append((loc, text, Path(reel).name, size, crop.stat().st_size))
    print(f"[{loc}] reel={Path(reel).name} ({size} B)  text={text!r}  crop={crop.name} ({crop.stat().st_size} B)")

print("\nALL REELS GENERATED:", all(r[3] > 0 and r[4] > 0 for r in results))
